### [1] [An Executable Benchmark for LLM-Based HLS Repair:Design Complexity and Repair Underconstraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03971)
*Maisha Mastora,Dean Sullivan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文首次全面评估大语言模型修复 HLS 设计的能力，构建了可执行修复基准并提出新的难度度量指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅报告 GPT-4 修复准确率低，但未分析修复失败原因及难度驱动因素。

Method: 评估四个模型在 125 个基准、8 种 bug 类型上的表现；构建含特定功能预言机的可执行 APR 基准，采用 pass@k 评估。

Result: 设计与规模主导难度而非 bug 类型；提出解多重性指标预测失败；前沿模型 pass@1 达 73-76%。

Conclusion: 未来 APR 基准应包含复杂、可执行且难以识别的设计，以确保持续挑战前沿模型。

Abstract: Automated repair of High-Level Synthesis (HLS) designs using large language models (LLMs) is an emerging but underexplored problem. While LLM-based repair shows strong results on register-transfer level (RTL) Verilog, the only prior systematic study of HLS logic repair reports just 10.5% correction accuracy for GPT-4, with no analysis of why repair fails or what drives difficulty. We present the first comprehensive evaluation of LLM-based HLS repair across four models (GPT-4o, GPT-4o-mini, GPT-5.4, and Claude Opus 4.6) on 125 benchmark instances spanning eight logic bug types across three open-source HLS suites (CHStone, MachSuite, Polybench). We construct the first executable APR-style HLS repair benchmark with suite-specific functional oracles, enabling pass@k evaluation rather than the string-match approximations used in prior work. Design context and scale, rather than bug type alone, dominate repair difficulty: repair rates range from 6-45% on complex cryptographic kernels (CHStone) to 81-93% on compact algorithmic kernels (MachSuite) despite identical bug type distributions. We introduce solution multiplicity, the fraction of distinct patches generated across repeated repair attempts, as an empirical measure of repair underconstraint, and show it strongly predicts repair failure across all four models (Spearman rho = -0.393, p = 6.61 x 10^-20). Frontier models reduce underconstrained instances from 41-46 (GPT-4o, GPT-4o-mini) to just 3 (Claude Opus 4.6), with pass@1 improving from 42-53% to 73-76%. SHFT bugs remain consistently hard across all models, and semantic bug classes such as buffer indexing become reliably repairable only at frontier scale. These findings show that future APR benchmarks must include complex, executable, and weakly identifiable designs that remain challenging after frontier LLM repair.

</details>


### [2] [Alkaid: A Compiler Framework for Ultra-Low-Latency Kernels on Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04808)
*Chang Sun,Zhiqiang Que,Dimitrios Danopoulos,Maurizio Pierini,Wayne Luk,Maria Spiropulu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Alkaid是一个开源领域特定编译器，用于将亚微秒级延迟的机器学习数据流内核编译为RTL或HLS代码，实现更低的资源占用和延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ML到硬件的流程在超低延迟场景下缺乏细粒度位宽控制、算术优化和快速性能估计，难以满足亚微秒级延迟需求。

Method: 提出Alkaid编译器，核心是算术中心的中间表示ALIR，保留定点语义、异构位宽和操作级结构；提供硬件感知优化passes和白盒分析性能模型，支持无综合的设计空间探索。

Result: 在MLP、GNN、Transformer等典型ML负载上，Alkaid比最佳先前流程降低20-30% LUT使用，延迟最高降低40%；在提升决策树综合上达到最先进水平，并支持排序网络、直方图等非ML内核。

Conclusion: Alkaid通过算术感知的IR和快速性能模型，显著提升了超低延迟ML流水线的硬件实现效率，扩展了适用范围至非ML内核，为快速原型设计提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Ultra-low-latency machine learning and data processing pipelines operating on the sub-microsecond level often contain static dataflow kernels that require fine-grained bitwidth control, arithmetic optimization, and fast hardware performance estimation. This works introduce Alkaid, a free and open source domain specific compiler that translates sub-microsecond latency dataflow kernels into platform agnostic Register Transfer Level (RTL) or High-Level Synthesis (HLS)-ready code in seconds. Alkaid targets ultra-low-latency ML pipelines, especially those addressed by existing ML-to-hardware flows such as \texttt{hls4ml}, while also supporting the surrounding pre-processing, post-processing, and control logic. At its core, Alkaid uses an arithmetic centric intermediate representation, Alkaid Low-level IR (ALIR), to preserve fixed-point semantics, heterogeneous bitwidths, and operation level structure for optimization and code generation. Alkaid further provides comprehensive hardware-aware optimization passes and an interpretable white-box analytical performance model for rapid design space exploration without synthesis in the loop. Across representative ML workloads, including MLPs, GNNs, and transformers, Alkaid achieves 20-30\% lower LUT usage than the best prior flows for bit-exact equivalent designs, with up to 40\% lower latency in some cases. Beyond typical neural networks, Alkaid achieves comparable performance to the state-of-the-art method for synthesizing boosted decision trees, while also enabling the implementation and integration of non-ML kernels such as sorting networks and histograms.

</details>


### [3] [SparseCraft: Agentic Hardware-Software Co-Optimization for Sparse Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05037)
*Rajatabha Chakraborty,M P Samartha,Vedant Pahariya,Priyesh Shukla*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SparseCraft 通过语言模型驱动闭环硬件自动化循环，填补了稀疏加速器设计搜索中模型预测与硬件实际表现之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稀疏加速器设计空间通常基于分析模型进行搜索，设计点的采纳取决于模型预测而非硬件实际运行结果。

Method: 语言模型在 15 次迭代中读取测量结果，经 MCP 工具服务器编辑 Chisel RTL、内存配置和内核调度。候选设计需经合法性检查、综合、周期精确仿真、与黄金参考逐位比对等严格验证，另有第二模型修复失败的修改。

Result: 在 512x512 GraphChallenge 稀疏 DNN 层上，相比块稀疏 Gemmini 基线，循环数减少 2.1 倍，片外流量减少 9.8 倍，面积减少 22.8%，每瓦性能提高 5.61 倍，EDP 降低 11.8 倍。

Conclusion: 该方法通过调度优化、零门控 MAC 及跳过单元、内存调整三个层面有效优化了性能与能效，验证了语言模型闭环自动设计的有效性。

Abstract: Sparse-accelerator design spaces are usually searched against analytical models, so a design point is admitted on what a model predicts rather than on what the hardware does. SparseCraft closes that gap with a language model inside a closed CHIA loop. In each of 15 iterations the model reads the measured outcome of the previous one and edits the Chisel RTL, the memory configuration and the sparse-kernel schedule of a Gemmini accelerator through MCP tool servers, and no candidate counts until it has been checked for legality, elaborated, simulated cycle-accurately, checked bit-for-bit on every output against a golden reference, and synthesised. The harness turns each measurement into the next work order, a diagnosed bottleneck with matching strategy guidance, the history of tried designs and a score of the model's own prediction, and a second model repairs changes that fail a gate. On a $512 \times 512$ GraphChallenge sparse-DNN layer the loop reaches 2.1x fewer cycles, 9.8x less off-chip traffic and 22.8% less area than the block-sparse Gemmini baseline, with 5.61x higher modelled perf/W and 11.8x lower EDP. The levers span three layers: a schedule that keeps the dense operand resident removes 9.8x of the traffic, a zero-gated MAC and a zero-row skip unit that the model wrote in Chisel cut energy, and resizing the memories cuts area.

</details>


### [4] [Beyond LLM Serving: Characterizing Vision-Language-Action Workloads for Embodied AI System Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05062)
*Seonghun Jung,Sieun Moon,Jiyoung Jeong,Jimin Lee,Jaehyuk Huh*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文 characterization 四种代表性 VLA 模型在边缘 GPU 服务器和两个 onboard SoC 上的运行时行为, 发现动作张量维度决定阶段是内存还是计算受限, 平台平衡和 GPU 频率扩展会改变瓶颈, 并在闭环操作中提出了 accuracy-speed-energy 的权衡; 指导了 VLA 模型架构、硬件和运行时策略的联合设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型在机器人操作中将多模态观测转化为低层次动作, 每个控制周期设有推理截止时间, 超时会导致机器人基于陈旧观测行动, 降低任务成功率。为满足截止时间, 亟需在设备本地或边缘部署, 但单机器人 batch-1 推理场景超出了 LLM 服务系统的设计点, 因此需要深入理解 VLA 架构在这一特定运行环境下的性能表现。

Method: 在边缘 GPU 服务器和两个 onboard SoC 上对四种代表性 VLA 模型进行性能 characterization, 使用单次推理 profiling 和 43,200 轮闭环实验。分析动作张量维度对内存/计算受限的影响, 考察平台平衡和 GPU 频率扩展对瓶颈的改变, 并研究推理与动作执行重叠下的 accuracy-speed-energy 权衡。

Result: 动作张量维度决定推理阶段是内存还是计算受限; 平台平衡可改变该瓶颈; GPU 频率扩展存在平台相关的最佳能效-延迟点。闭环运行中, 推理与动作执行的重叠产生 accuracy-speed-energy 权衡, 且没有任何配置在所有部署 SLO 下都是帕累托占优。

Conclusion: 所得结果指导了 VLA 模型架构、硬件选择和运行时策略的联合设计, 为在边缘设备上高效部署 VLA 模型提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models translate multimodal observations into low-level robot actions. During robot operation, each control period sets an inference deadline, and overruns leave the robot acting on stale observations, reducing task success. Meeting this deadline motivates on-device or nearby edge execution, where a single robot requires batch-1 inference outside the design point of LLM serving systems. Although VLA architectures combine familiar vision-language, autoregressive, and diffusion-style components, their runtime behavior in this batch-1 control setting remains uncharacterized. We characterize four representative VLA models on an edge GPU server and two onboard SoCs, using single-inference profiling and 43,200 closed-loop episodes. Action tensor dimensionality determines whether a stage is memory- or compute-bound, platform balance can shift that bottleneck, and GPU frequency scaling yields a platform-dependent energy-latency sweet spot. In closed-loop operation, overlapping inference with action execution creates an accuracy-speed-energy tradeoff, and no configuration is Pareto-dominant across deployment SLOs. These results guide joint design of VLA model architectures, hardware, and runtime policies.

</details>


### [5] [An ASIC Design Flow for High-Level Synthesis of Asynchronous Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05112)
*Mayuresh Warunjikar,Madhav Desai*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究使用 AHIR HLS 框架把指令式程序转换为异步电路，控制路径采用 1‑safe、live 的受限 Petri 网，数据路径为单轨并采用 bundled‑data 交互，形式化推导了单侧必要且充分的时序约束，并提供静态时序分析器进行验证；最终展示了 AES 加密算法的端到端实现，通过后布局仿真确认正确性并评估性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业级 EDA 对高级综合的异步系统支持不足，尤其缺少从指令式程序到异步电路的自动化路径，而异步电路具备低空闲功耗、低延迟和无需时钟分发等优势，亟需一种可与现成 ASIC 流程兼容的综合方法。

Method: 采用 AHIR 框架，将控制路径建模为 1‑safe 且 live 的受限 Petri 网；生成延迟不敏感的控制器和单轨数据路径，使用 bundled‑data 实现控制器与数据路径的交互，类似 micropipelines 架构；形式化推导并证明所需时序约束是单侧的（可行）、必要且充分；开发静态时序分析器检查约束满足度；整合到现成的 ASIC 工具链和标准同步单元库中。

Result: 以 AES 加密算法为案例，实现端到端的异步电路，后布局电路仿真验证功能正确，并给出性能估算（如吞吐量、功耗），表明所生成电路满足所推导的时序约束并在实际工艺下可工作。

Conclusion: 该工作首次展示了从指令式程序通过 AHIR 自动合成具有形式时序保证的异步电路的完整流程，验证了其可行性与性能优势，为工业级异步系统的 EDA 支持提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Asynchronous circuits have some benefits over their synchronous counterparts, such as lower idle power consumption, potentially lower latency, absence of clock distribution issues, among others. However, industry-scale electronic design automation (EDA) support, particularly for high-level synthesis (HLS) of asynchronous systems from imperative programs, remains limited. We present an approach to synthesize asynchronous circuits from imperative programs using the AHIR HLS framework. The framework models the control path in the form of a restricted form of Petri nets that are 1-safe and live by construction. In this work, we start with this representation in AHIR and generate asynchronous circuits with a delay insensitive controller, a single-rail data path with bundled-data interaction between the controller and the data path that resembles the micropipelines architecture. We formalize the timing constraints required for correct functioning of the generated circuit and show that those constraints are one sided (feasible), necessary and sufficient. We have also developed a static timing analyzer to check that the circuit meets these constraints. Our flow integrates seamlessly with off-the-shelf ASIC toolchains and standard synchronous cell libraries. To demonstrate our approach, we show an end to end implementation of an AES encryption algorithm and use post layout circuit simulation to verify the correctness and estimate the performance of the resultant circuit.

</details>


### [6] [Reinforcement Learning-Based Optimization of Workload-Aware Power Delivery Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06148)
*Oran Hayes,Maria Pantazi-Kypraiou,Athanasios Tziouvaras,George Stamoulis,Anuj Pathania,Shreejith Shanker,George Floros*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出基於強化學習的工作負載感知電源交付網路(PDN)最佳化框架，利用系統層模擬取得之架構功率軌跡，映射為空間功率密度分布，並透過Deep Q-Network(DQN)強化學習代理人進行導線寬度最佳化，以最小化PDN面積同時滿足電氣和可靠性限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 傳統PDN設計方法多基於最壞情況假設，導致電源網路過度配置與資源浪費；而Modern VLSI晶片需在穩壓、電遷移(EM)與IR-drop限制下，具備工作負載感知能力，以提升效率與可靠性。

Method: 首先利用系統層模擬生成工作負載感知PDN，將架構功率軌跡映射為空間功率密度分布以實現局部電流需求之自適應資源分配；接著採用DQN強化學習代理人進行導線寬度最佳化；並透過SPICE電路分析與EM終壽估算取得電氣與可靠性指標。

Result: 在4、8及16核心多處理器平面配置之PARSEC與SPLASH-2基準工作負載資料集上驗證：DQN最佳化器平均将Normalized PDN面積降低47%，滿足所有EM與IR-drop限制；與模擬退火相比，最佳化品質相當且速度約快26倍。

Conclusion: 提出之強化學習PDN最佳化框架能有效減少面積配置，同時保持電氣與可靠性約束，且具顯著的效率優勢，為工作負載感知電源設計提供有效方法。

Abstract: Power Delivery Networks (PDNs) are critical components of modern VLSI chips, providing stable voltage levels while satisfying electromigration (EM) and IR-drop constraints. Conventional PDN design methodologies typically rely on worst-case assumptions, often resulting in over-provisioned networks and inefficient use of resources. This paper presents a reinforcement learning-based framework for the optimization of workload-aware PDNs. The proposed methodology first generates workload-aware PDNs using architectural power traces obtained from system-level simulations. These power traces are mapped to spatial power density distributions, enabling adaptive allocation of PDN resources according to local current demand. A reinforcement learning agent then performs wire-width optimization to minimize PDN area while maintaining EM and voltage integrity constraints. Electrical and reliability metrics are obtained using SPICE-based circuit analysis and EM lifetime estimation. Experimental evaluation is performed on a dataset of workload-aware PDNs generated from 4-, 8-, and 16-core multiprocessor floorplans using PARSEC and SPLASH-2 benchmark workloads. Furthermore, the proposed Deep Q-Network (DQN)-based optimizer reduces the average normalized PDN area by 47\% while satisfying all EM and IR-drop constraints. Compared to simulated annealing, the proposed approach achieves comparable optimization quality while providing approximately 26$\times$ faster optimization.

</details>


### [7] [Terracotta: Enabling the Adoption of New DRAM Techniques via a Flexible DRAM Interface and Memory Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06475)
*Harsh Songara,Konstantinos Kanellopoulos,F. Nisa Bostancı,Konstantinos Marios Sgouras,Ataberk Olgun,İsmail Emir Yüksel,Andreas Kosmas Kakolyris,A. Giray Yağlıkçı,Onur Mutlu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Terracotta 是一个灵活的框架，通过自定义命令扩展和可编程内存控制器，使多种 DRAM 技术无需重复修改接口和控制器即可部署，在保留 >96% 性能收益的同时仅引入极低开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 DRAM 技术各自需要修改僵化的 DRAM 接口和内存控制器，部署困难，且不同技术的命令和控制器结构有相似性，可利用这一点减少重复修改。

Method: 提出 Terracotta 框架，包含两个灵活组件：(i) 自定义命令扩展，让 DRAM 厂商在标准化接口内定义新命令；(ii) 可编程内存控制器，让系统设计师在后硅阶段编程支持新技术。基于 DDR5 系统设计并评估。

Result: 对于四个不同领域的 DRAM 技术，Terracotta 保留了定制实现 >96% 的性能收益；两种技术的组合优于单独实现；开销极低：DRAM 能量 0.6-3.2%，面积 0.03%，功耗 0.56%。

Conclusion: Terracotta 通过标准化接口和可编程控制器，实现了多种 DRAM 技术的低成本、高效率部署，显著降低了硬件修改复杂度，并已开源。

Abstract: DRAM continues to limit the performance, energy efficiency, and robustness of modern systems. Many prior works propose DRAM techniques that support in-DRAM computation, improve memory access latency and parallelism, and enhance DRAM maintenance and reliability. However, adopting each new technique requires repeated modifications to the rigid DRAM interface and memory controller, hindering its deployment. Our goal is to reduce these repeated modifications. We observe that the DRAM commands and memory controller structures of many DRAM techniques are similar. Our key idea is to use these similarities to compose a set of primitives for implementing diverse DRAM techniques. We propose Terracotta, a new framework with two flexible components: (i) custom command extensions that let DRAM vendors define new commands within a single, standardized interface, and (ii) a programmable memory controller that system designers can program to support new DRAM techniques post-silicon. Together, these enable deployment by configuring the memory controller instead of modifying the interface and controller. We design Terracotta for a DDR5-based system and evaluate its performance, energy, and hardware complexity. For four DRAM techniques from four distinct domains (processing-using-DRAM, low-cost DRAM maintenance, subarray-level parallelism, and latency reduction), Terracotta retains almost all of the performance benefits (>96%) of custom implementations. A Terracotta-based composition of two techniques outperforms the Terracotta-based implementation of each technique alone, demonstrating the benefits of adding techniques without repeated interface and controller modifications. Terracotta incurs low DRAM energy (0.6-3.2%), area (0.03%), and power (0.56%) overheads in a high-end server-grade processor. Terracotta's source code is freely available at https://github.com/CMU-SAFARI/Terracotta.

</details>

### [8] [Smells and Refactorings for Software Environmental Sustainability: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03838)
*Ornela Danushi,Flavio Sampirisi,Jacopo Soldani,Stefano Forti,Antonio Brogi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对“绿色异味”及其重构方法的系统文献综述，识别并分类了20种影响软件环境可持续性的异味，并提供了对应的重构映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICT的环境影响日益严重，软件系统中的结构性低效（即“绿色异味”）会导致不必要的能源消耗，需要通过识别这些异味并实施重构来降低软件的生态足迹。

Method: 采用系统文献综述法，分析和分类了截至2026年3月发表的66篇关于绿色异味及其重构技术的文献。

Result: 识别出20种影响软件环境可持续性的绿色异味，并为每种异味关联了一种或多种能减轻其环境负面影响的重构技术。

Conclusion: 本研究整理的绿色异味及对应重构目录，能为从业者在日常开发中提供实用价值，同时也为科研人员在可持续软件工程领域开展新研究提供了起点。

Abstract: Context: The environmental impact of ICT is constantly growing. Enhancing its environmental sustainability requires dealing with energy-hungry software systems, by possibly reducing their footprint. Objective: To better frame the problem and its persistent nature, software can be viewed as carrying structural inefficiencies regardless of how advanced its functionality may be. Identifying these inefficiencies as "green smells" provides a basis for refactoring code to mitigate its ecological impact. We aim at identifying well-known green smells impacting environmental sustainable software and map them to corresponding refactoring aimed at reducing the impact therein. Method: In this article, we conduct a systematic literature review on the articles that discuss green smells and their possible refactorings. We analyse and classify 66 primary studies published up to March 2026. Results: We identify 20 smells impacting environmental sustainability of software. Each smell is associated with one or more refactoring techniques known to mitigate their negative effects on the environmental sustainability of software. Conclusions: The literature witnesses the interest in resolving the green smells affecting software systems. The catalogued green smells and the corresponding refactorings can provide pragmatic value for practitioners in their daily development workflows, while also offering researchers a starting point to establish new research directions in sustainable software engineering.

</details>


### [9] [Characterizing Open-Source Video Games from a Software Engineering Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03953)
*Henrique Mandelli Canella,Yohan Duarte,Cristiano Politowski,Vinicius Durelli,Andre Takeshi Endo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究从软件工程视角对开源视频游戏进行了特征刻画，分析了491款来自itch.io并在GitHub上开源的视频游戏，揭示了其类型分布、仓库活性、引擎选择及测试实践等特征，旨在为独立游戏开发者和小型团队提供实践启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频游戏市场庞大，独立开发者和小型团队常通过itch.io发布游戏并开源代码。这些开源项目为实证软件工程研究提供了宝贵且透明的语料库，但目前缺乏对其软件工程特征（如开发活跃度、测试实践等）的系统性刻画。

Method: 研究通过提取itch.io上发布的、并在GitHub上开源的491款视频游戏的元数据，从类型、流行度（玩家评分）、仓库活性、实现特征（如游戏引擎）以及测试实践文档化程度等多个维度进行了定量分析。

Result: 结果显示：开源游戏主要集中在解谜（Puzzle）、动作（Action）和平台（Platformer）等有限类型；玩家评分普遍较高；大部分仓库处于休眠或低维护状态；绝大多数项目采用Godot或Unity引擎；测试相关实践的文档化程度极低。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了开源视频游戏在开发维护、技术选型和测试实践方面的现状与挑战。这些发现为理解独立游戏开发者的软件工程需求提供了实证基础，有助于推动针对小型团队的专用工具和工程实践的发展。

Abstract: Video games hold a prominent role in the entertainment industry, which constitutes a multibillion-dollar global market. Within this ecosystem, independent studios, small teams, and solo developers operate with constrained budgets while making their games available through platforms such as itch.io, where many projects are also available as open-source. Where even if the project is fully released, other developers can still have access to the development process through the source code, helping other developers to develop their own games. These open-source games also constitute a valuable corpus for empirical software engineering research, offering transparent access to development artifacts that are typically inaccessible in commercial projects. This study characterizes open-source video games from a Software Engineering standpoint, focusing on their genres, popularity, repository activity, implementation characteristics, and testing practices. To this end, we extracted metadata from 491 video games from itch.io, published as open-source projects in GitHub. The results indicate that open-source games predominantly fall within limited genres (Puzzle, Action, and Platformer), that player ratings, when available, tend to be high, bringing a perception that the community receives well those kind of games, that repositories are largely dormant or minimally maintained, that the majority of these projects adopt either Godot or Unity as their game engine, and that documentation of testing-related practices is seldom included. The results offer insights into the development of open-source video games and may contribute to advancing software engineering practices and tools designed for small teams and independent game developers.

</details>


### [10] [How Do Coding Agents Optimize Software and Report Performance Validation? A Large-Scale Empirical Study of Open-Source Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03969)
*Huiyun Peng,Ricardo Calvo,Kelechi G. Kalu,James C. Davis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究对比了1,130个AI代理和1,130个人类提交的性能优化PR，发现AI代理PR合并率较低，但两者优化策略和验证率相似。AI代理更依赖静态推理，但基准测试使用随时间收敛。整体上，两组均缺乏严格的量化证据，凸显了改进评估基础设施的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI编码代理在开源项目中提交大量性能优化PR，但尚不清楚这些变更是否符合成熟的优化实践，以及其价值论证是否充分。本研究旨在揭示AI代理与人类在优化实践和验证行为上的差异与差距。

Method: 扩展MSR 2026试点研究，收集了截至2026年6月的1,130个AI代理和1,130个人类编写的性能PR。系统对比了两组的合并结果、补丁特征、优化策略（如静态推理与基准测试）以及验证行为。

Result: AI代理PR的合并率低于人类（54.4% vs 73.3%），但两者优化策略和验证率相近。AI代理更依赖静态推理、较少使用基准测试（46.1% vs 57.2%），但随时间推移趋于收敛。两组中均有约半数已验证的PR缺乏量化指标，且极少对性能权衡进行量化。

Conclusion: AI代理的优化实践虽逐渐向人类实践靠拢，但底层证据依然不完整，缺乏严格的量化和多维证据。研究呼吁建立衡量预期效果和相关成本的评估基础设施与审查标准，不应仅将“存在验证”视为充分条件。

Abstract: Software performance optimization requires identifying bottlenecks and improvement opportunities, and empirically evaluating the effects and trade-offs across performance objectives. Autonomous AI coding agents submit performance-oriented pull requests (PRs) to open-source projects, yet it remains unclear whether these changes reflect established optimization practices and adequately substantiate their claimed value. Extending our MSR 2026 Mining Challenge pilot study of 407 performance PRs, we analyze a sample of 1,130 agentic and 1,130 human-authored performance PRs collected through June 2026. We compare adoption outcomes and patch characteristics between the two groups, and examine their optimization practices and validation behaviors. The results show that agentic PRs are merged less often than human-authored PRs (54.4% vs. 73.3%), but the two groups use similar optimization strategies and report validation at comparable rates. Agentic PRs rely more on static reasoning and less on benchmarks (46.1% vs. 57.2%), although benchmark use converges by the end of the study period. Agentic PRs also report validation for code-smell refactorings as often as for resource-targeting changes, whereas human-authored PRs do so less often. Across both groups, about half of validated PRs report no quantitative performance metric, and relevant trade-offs are rarely quantified. Agentic optimization practice has become increasingly similar to human practice, but the supporting evidence remains incomplete. Establishing whether agent-proposed optimizations are supported by rigorous, quantitative, and multidimensional evidence remains an important challenge. These findings motivate evaluation infrastructure and review criteria that measure intended effects and relevant costs rather than treating the presence of validation alone as sufficient.

</details>


### [11] [World Requirement Model: Learning Requirement-Change Consequences from Typed Artifact Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04445)
*Yuanpeng He,Lijian Li,Dongming Jin,Huanyao Zhang,Fangjing Li,Linyu Li,Chung-ju Huang,Tianxiang Zhan,Qingsong Wen,Wenpin Jiao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 World Requirement Model (WRM)，将需求变更的工程上下文建模为带类型的制品图，利用关系感知注意力和类型传播预测影响、冲突、违规和缺陷风险等多任务指标，在合成数据集上显著优于基线 MLP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求变更会波及利益相关者、约束、组件和测试，现有方法难以捕捉制品间的复杂关系和动态影响，因而需要一种能够建模全局制品图并进行情境化后果评估的统一框架。

Method: WRM 采用带类型的制品图表示工程上下文，使用关系感知注意力对节点进行上下文编码，并通过类型传播机制在图中传递信息；引入世界表示和决策表示以学习动态变化；共享读出层分别预测影响、冲突、违规和缺陷风险的得分，并通过辅助目标监督后继邻接和 latent 预测；训练时采用 scored cases 进行检点选择，回骨网络保持未匹配以隔离模型效果。

Result: 在六个领域的 282 个合成案例中的 28 个有分数案例上，WRM 的影响 mean average precision (MAP) 达到 0.724，而基线的 hashed‑text MLP 为 0.623，相对提升 16.2%，配对差值 0.101（95% 置信区间 [0.044,0.159]）；最低四分位的 mean AP 提升 32.4%，同领域 MAP 提升 13.3%；在扩充语料上的四组 47 案比较中，MAP 增幅在 15.6%–29.1% 之间，所有五个报告指标均呈现更高均值。

Conclusion: WRM 提供了一种制品寻址的世界模型 formulation，验证了上下文感知后果评分的有效性，并给出了评估需求‑世界预测的显式条件；研究进一步分析了候选覆盖界限及线性影响头的局限，为后续模型改进指明方向。

Abstract: Requirement changes can affect connected stakeholders, constraints, components, and tests. We present World Requirement Model (WRM), which encodes this engineering context as a typed artifact graph and predicts consequences at shared artifact identifiers. Relation-aware attention and typed propagation contextualize nodes; world and decision representations support learned dynamics. Shared readouts score impact, conflict, violation, and defect risk; auxiliary objectives supervise successor adjacency and latent prediction. On 28 scored cases from 282 synthetic cases in six domains, WRM obtains impact mean average precision (MAP) of 0.724 versus 0.623 for a hashed-text multilayer perceptron (MLP), a 16.2\% relative gain and paired difference of 0.101 (conditional 95\% interval [0.044,0.159]). Lowest-quarter mean AP improves by 32.4\%, and equal-domain MAP by 13.3\%. Four 47-case comparisons on an expanded corpus show MAP gains of 15.6--29.1\% and higher means on all five reported metrics. The recorded advantage thus extends across score summaries and annotation/training settings. Checkpoints were selected on scored cases, and backbones are unmatched, so these results characterize selected systems. Our analysis establishes candidate-coverage bounds and shows that the current linear impact head cannot rerank a fixed world's artifacts across decisions. WRM contributes an artifact-addressed world-model formulation, comparative evidence for contextual consequence scoring, and explicit conditions for evaluating requirement-world prediction.

</details>


### [12] [Noise-Resilient Testing of Quantum Programs Based on Output Dominance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04461)
*Yuechen Li,Yige Yang,Tao Yue*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Qomino is a new quantum software testing method that directly judges test correctness from noisy measurement results, without separate noise estimation or mitigation. It uses decomposition-aware inverse construction, compares outcomes to the strongest observed competitor, and uses bounded retries for inconclusive cases. Tested on six Qiskit programs with 30 buggy variants across six simulated noisy backends, Qomino achieves 85.65% overall accuracy (99.95% on a subset) and runs faster than baseline statistical, quantum-specific, and learning-based methods.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional quantum software testing struggles with noise in the NISQ era, requiring costly noise modeling or mitigation that depends on calibration data or backend specifics. A method that can reliably judge test outcomes from noisy samples without such preprocessing is needed.

Method: Qomino employs dominance‑guided testing: it builds inverse models aware of program decomposition, compares expected test outcomes against the strongest observed competitor, and uses bounded retry logic to resolve ambiguous decisions, avoiding an explicit noise estimation or mitigation step.

Result: Evaluated on 30 buggy variants from six Qiskit programs across six simulated noisy backends, Qomino attains 85.65% overall test accuracy and 99.95% accuracy for two of the programs. It also demonstrates superior execution speed compared to six baseline approaches (statistical, quantum‑specific, and learning‑based). Ablation studies confirm the effectiveness of each core component.

Conclusion: Qomino provides a practical, fast, and accurate quantum software testing solution for noisy NISQ devices, outperforming existing baselines while eliminating the need for separate noise handling.

Abstract: Quantum software testing (QST) is a central quality-assurance activity for checking whether quantum programs conform to their specifications. In the noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) era, gate, decoherence, and measurement errors distort observed outputs, making it difficult to distinguish program defects from noise. The existing noise-aware QST literature typically mitigates or models noise before applying a test oracle. However, these strategies can incur substantial cost and depend on calibration data, model training, or backend-specific information, limiting their applicability. We present Qomino, a dominance-guided QST approach for judging tests from noisy samples without a separate noise-estimation or mitigation phase. The approach combines decomposition-aware inverse construction, comparison of the expected outcome with the strongest observed competitor, and bounded retries for inconclusive decisions. We evaluate Qomino on 30 controlled buggy variants from six Qiskit programs and six simulated noisy backends, comparing it with six baselines spanning statistical, quantum-specific, and learning-based methods. Qomino achieves 85.65% accuracy over all six programs and 99.95% in the restricted two-program evaluation, significantly outperforming the corresponding baselines. Across the evaluated programs and backends under the stated settings, Qomino is faster than the compared quantum-specific and learning-based methods. Ablation and retry analyses support the contributions of its main components.

</details>


### [13] [DreamTest: World-Model Surrogates for Search-Based Testing of Deep Reinforcement Learning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04494)
*Qinghua Xu,Guancheng Wang,Boxi Yu,Liting Lin,Lionel Briand*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DreamTest is a world‑model surrogate that learns agent behavior and environment dynamics from training logs, then simulates test episodes to predict failures. It dramatically outperforms black‑box baselines in failure prediction (up to 97% higher AUPRC), discovers more novel failures (up to 79% gains) and covers a broader range of behavioral clusters, enabling cheaper and more diverse testing of DRL agents in cyber‑physical systems.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Testing DRL agents in cyber‑physical systems is expensive because each execution can be costly. Existing surrogate‑assisted methods treat the system as a black box and predict pass/fail outcomes directly. This work instead models how a test unfolds via imagined rollouts to estimate failures more accurately and guide search without executing every candidate.

Method: DreamTest employs a recurrent state‑space model trained on an agent’s training logs to capture both the agent’s policy and the environment dynamics. For a candidate test configuration, the surrogate performs imagined rollouts, producing a failure score that drives the search. The surrogate is evaluated for failure prediction, test generation, and failure diversity on three benchmarks (Parking, Humanoid, DonkeyCar).

Result: DreamTest achieves mean AUPRC improvements of 97%, 12%, and 39% over the strongest baseline on the three domains. Out‑of‑distribution test‑set gains reach 145%, 29%, and 44%. Under the same simulator‑validation budget, the best "DreamTest + search" combinations find 29%, 22%, and 79% more novel failures on average. Across clusterings with k = 2‑40, failures generated with DreamTest cover the most behavioral clusters for nearly all k, indicating consistent discovery of diverse failures.

Conclusion: DreamTest provides an effective, scalable surrogate‑assisted testing framework that predicts and generates diverse failures with far lower execution cost. It improves both the efficiency and comprehensiveness of DRL agent testing in cyber‑physical systems.

Abstract: Testing deep reinforcement learning (DRL) agents in cyber-physical systems aims to uncover diverse failures before deployment, but each execution can be expensive. Surrogate-assisted testing reduces this cost by learning to predict which test configurations are likely to fail. Prior surrogates treat the system as a black box and predict pass or fail outcomes directly; we instead model how a test unfolds and estimate failure from an imagined episode.
  We introduce DreamTest, a world-model surrogate for testing DRL agents. DreamTest adapts a recurrent state-space model to learn agent behaviour and environment dynamics from the agent's training log. Given a candidate configuration, imagined rollouts produce a failure score that guides search without executing every candidate in a simulator or real system.
  We evaluate DreamTest for failure prediction, test generation, and failure diversity on Parking, Humanoid, and DonkeyCar. Mean area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) exceeds the strongest baseline by 97%, 12%, and 39%, respectively, and gains on five out-of-distribution test sets reach 145%, 29%, and 44%. Under the same simulator-validation budget, the best "DreamTest + search" combinations find 29%, 22%, and 79% more novel failures on average. Across clusterings with k = 2-40, failures generated with DreamTest cover the most behavioural clusters for almost all k, indicating that DreamTest consistently discovers behaviourally diverse failures.

</details>


### [14] [Practical Runtime Enforcement of First-Order Temporal Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04611)
*François Hublet,David Basin,Srđan Krstić*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出高效算法和工具EnfFlash，用于强制执行复杂时序需求，显著降低运行时延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有支持复杂策略的强制机制往往太慢，无法用于生产软件；开发者被迫采用非时序访问控制和临时插桩，但这些方法随需求复杂度扩展性差。

Method: 确定一个可在低运行时复杂度下强制的MFOTL片段，设计相应强制算法，将需求编译为命令式程序并高效解释，实现EnfFlash工具。

Result: 在标准基准上EnfFlash延迟比EnfGuard低44倍，在LLM代理安全策略上低达三个数量级；在社交网络上强制400行MFOTL GDPR公式每页面增加<15ms。

Conclusion: EnfFlash实现了复杂时序需求的高效强制，性能足以支持大多数交互式和实时应用，展示了实际部署的可行性。

Abstract: Runtime enforcers observe a system's behavior and exert control over it to ensure that the system always adheres to its requirements. Many natural requirements not only formulate restrictions on the system's present behavior, but also impose obligations on its future behavior; for instance, agentic security may require personal data collected during a run to be erased by some deadline. In an ever-growing compliance landscape, real-world systems may be subject to hundreds of such requirements. However, existing enforcement mechanisms supporting complex policies are often too slow for production software; instead, developers may resort to non-temporal access control mechanisms and ad-hoc instrumentation, but these scale poorly with requirement complexity.
  To address this problem, we introduce an efficient algorithm and tool for enforcing complex temporal requirements and demonstrate its performance. Specifically, we identify a fragment of Metric First-Order Temporal Logic (MFOTL) that can be enforced with low runtime complexity while supporting a rich family of practically relevant requirements, including obligations. We then design an enforcement algorithm for this fragment and implement it in EnfFlash, a novel tool that enforces requirements with latency up to 44x lower than EnfGuard, the previous state-of-the-art enforcer, on standard benchmarks, and up to three orders of magnitude lower on security policies for LLM agents. This performance is achieved by compiling requirements into imperative programs that we efficiently interpret. We evaluate EnfFlash both standalone, on existing benchmarks, and integrated in web applications as an enforcement backend. We demonstrate that it can enforce a substantial 400-line MFOTL formula specifying the GDPR on a social network while adding under 15 ms to each page view, which is sufficient for most interactive and real-time applications.

</details>


### [15] [When Talk Isn't Code: Comparing LLM Agents That Simulate and Develop Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04639)
*Xiang Li,Aichun Huang,Mingming Zhang,Zheli Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文对比了 LLM 模拟网络协议实现与生成代码的安全性，发现口头知识无法保证代码实现，模拟模式仅作初步筛选，不能替代代码测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究分别评估 LLM 作为模拟器或代码生成器，且知识探针常高估生成代码的安全性，缺乏在同一模型下对比仿真模式、开发模式与知识基线的研究。

Method: 设计统一攻击套件与预言机评估框架，在四种协议实现上对 15 个 LLM 的仿真模式、开发模式和知识探针进行对比测试。

Result: 除 DNS 外攻击成功率较低；DNS 任务中仿真与开发模式攻击率分别为 13% 和 15%；补充缺失要求可将 DNS 攻击降至 34%；知识探针无法预测代码实际实现的检查项。

Conclusion: 仿真模式可筛选 82% 的真实 DNS 漏洞，但存在误报漏报，不能替代开发模式测试；即使模型能复述要求，仍需书面化安全规范。

Abstract: LLMs are used both to simulate network protocol implementations, as honeypots do, and to write them. Prior work evaluates the two uses separately, from a model's answers or conversations in one case and from its generated code in the other. We observe that a knowledge probe (asking the model which security checks an implementation needs) and a conversation can credit security checks that the generated program lacks, but no study has compared them with the code the same model writes. To fill this gap, we present the first such comparison between simulation mode (S-mode), where the model plays the implementation in a conversation, and development mode (D-mode), where the model writes the program and the program is attacked, with a knowledge probe as a baseline. We design and implement a harness that judges all three with one attack suite and one oracle, and evaluate 15 LLMs on four protocol implementations. We find that with the full security specification, the median model's attack success is at most 4\% on reassembly, HTTP, and firewall implementations in both modes, but 13\% in S-mode and 15\% in D-mode on DNS. In D-mode, adding the missing DNS requirement to the specification cuts that attack from 100\% to 34\% and changes only that check, whereas deleting a stated rule weakens the program on other checks too. Asking the model beforehand does not predict which checks a program has, since 13 of the 14 models that write a DNS resolver name the check, yet all 42 programs lack it. S-mode is a useful first filter, flagging 82\% of the real DNS vulnerabilities and leaving 96\% of the safe cases unflagged. It errs in both directions, over-reporting security checks that D-mode does not implement and under-reporting checks that D-mode does. S-mode can screen but not replace D-mode testing, and security requirements should be written down even when a model can recite them.

</details>


### [16] [Back to the Future: Regressing Readability Features from LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04641)
*Tairan Wang,Earl T. Barr*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: BTTF(Back To The Future)是一种结合线性回归和信息论特征的代码可读性评估模型。它使用传统代码度量、基于嵌入的语义组织和因果语言模型预测能力,来捕获源代码结构、语义连贯性和上下文惊新度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理的广泛使用使得代码可读性变得更加重要,可帮助人类审核代理生成的代码,并帮助代理在有限上下文窗口内维护代码。然而,现有的可读性模型在不同数据集上与人类判断的一致性不佳。

Method: BTTF将语言模型用作测量工具而非黑盒判断工具。它结合了三类特征:传统代码度量(捕获源结构),基于嵌入的语义组织(捕获语义连贯性),以及因果语言模型的可预测性(捕获上下文惊新度)。该模型在5个公开可用的人工标注可读性基准和手动标注的MBJP开发集上进行评估,共计1100个样本。

Result: BTTF在人类可读性判断上表现优异,平均Spearman相关系数比最强基线提高了多达0.101。在13种受控可读性降低转换下,Java响应率达到93.8%,Python达到87.6%,分别比Dorn提高了14%和19.2%。BTTF在本地模型中表现最佳,在Java和Python上分别仅落后于最强云LLM 1.7%和5.1%。直接LLM基线平均需要1083个提示词标记进行评估,且不透明。

Conclusion: BTTF通过结合多种信息论特征,实现了最先进的基准性能和强大的转换鲁棍性,同时提供了可解释的特征系数。每个特征系数都匹配其理论方向效应,使模型在性能和可解释性之间取得了良好的平衡。

Abstract: Code readability supports software understanding, review, and maintenance. As AI coding agents become more widely used, readable code helps both humans review agent-generated output and agents maintain code within limited context windows. Yet existing readability models do not consistently agree with human judgements across datasets.
  We introduce BTTF (Back To The Future), pairing linear regression with information-theoretic features. Rather than using language models as black-box judges, BTTF uses them as measurement instruments. It combines traditional code measurements, embedding-based semantic organisation, and causal-LM predictability to capture source structure, semantic coherence, and contextual surprise.
  We evaluate BTTF on five publicly available human-rated readability benchmarks and our manually annotated MBJP development set, totalling 1,100 samples. BTTF improves average Spearman correlation over the strongest baseline by up to 0.101. Across 13 controlled readability-reducing transformations on production code, it achieves response rates of 93.8% in Java and 87.6% in Python, outperforming Dorn by 14% and 19.2%. It performs best among local models and trails the strongest cloud-LLM response by 1.7% in Java and 5.1% in Python. The direct-LLM baselines require an average of 1,083 prompt tokens per evaluation while remaining opaque. BTTF combines state-of-the-art benchmark performance and strong transformation robustness with feature-granular interpretability: every feature coefficient matches its theoretical directional effect across all evaluated model configurations.

</details>


### [17] [RETRACE: From Entangled Repair Histories to Reusable Experience for CI Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04658)
*Rabeya Khatun Muna,Muhammad Ahasanuzzaman,Nakhla Rafi,Yisen Xu,Jinqiu Yang,Tse-Hsun Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RETRACE是一个框架，用于从软件历史中重建问题级别的修复经验。它结合了反向推理和前向追踪两种视角，通过CI执行证据来对齐问题与解决方案，从而实现历史CI修复作为可重用修复经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型代理经常重用先前经验，但大多数方法假设问题和解决方案已经对齐。然而，软件历史（如pull request）通常缺乏这种对齐——一个PR可能包含多个连续集成问题、失败尝试、回滚编辑和无关变更，使得难以确定哪些变更解决了哪个问题。

Method: RETRACE结合了两个视角：端点视图从保留在最终修订中的变更中反向推理；开发视图通过提交历史向前追踪修复演进。CI执行证据用于协调这两个视角，并将恢复的经验表示为三个抽象层次，从具体修复到可迁移的修复模式。

Result: 在CI-REPAIR-BENCH数据集上（包含101个仓库的565个PR级别修复，涵盖12类故障），MiniSWE-Agent的Pass@1准确率从MiniMax-M2.5时的19.6%提升至31.9%，从DeepSeek-V4-Flash时的23.3%提升至32.8%。Codex的Pass@1从15.5%提升至27.5%。同时使用双视角方法优于单独使用任一视角。

Conclusion: 通过恢复问题-变更对齐，RETRACE表明历史CI修复可以作为可重用的修复经验，这显著提高了LLM代理在新故障场景下的性能。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly reuse prior experience, but most approaches assume that problems and solutions are already aligned. Software histories rarely provide this alignment: a pull request (PR) may contain multiple continuous integration (CI) problems, failed attempts, reverted edits, and unrelated changes, obscuring which changes resolve each problem. We present RETRACE, a framework for reconstructing problem-level repair experience from such histories. RETRACE combines an endpoint view that reasons backward from changes retained in the passing revision with a development view that traces repair evolution forward through commit history. CI execution evidence reconciles the two views, and the recovered experience is represented at three abstraction levels, from concrete fixes to transferable repair patterns. For new failures, RETRACE retrieves relevant problem-level experience to guide repair. On CI-REPAIR-BENCH, comprising 565 PR-level repairs from 101 repositories across 12 failure categories, RETRACE improves mini-SWE-agent Pass@1 from 19.6% to 31.9% with MiniMax-M2.5 and from 23.3% to 32.8% with DeepSeek-V4-Flash. On a matched subset, Codex improves from 15.5% to 27.5%. Combining both views consistently outperforms either alone, showing that recovering problem-change alignment enables historical CI repairs to serve as reusable repair experience.

</details>


### [18] [Towards Automatically Pruning Logging Code with Coding Agents: How Far Are We?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04716)
*He Yang Yuan,Haonan Zhang,Xin Wang,An Ran Chen,Kisub Kim,Zishuo Ding,Zhenhao Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过构建LogRem数据集（387个真实案例）和评估四种编码代理，分析了开发者日志移除实践，发现自动化日志移除需要精确的范围判断，同时保持必要代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过多的日志会增加噪音、运行时开销并掩盖诊断信息。虽然之前的研究大量关注日志生成和修改，但日志移除仍被视为相对欠研究的领域。

Method: 从Python和Java仓库中提取并人工验证10个日志移除案例，推导出10种移除模式和11种移除原因；构建包含直接移除、日志基础设施移除和日志替换的LogRem数据集；评估四种编码代理，并比较其输出与实际变更对比。

Result: 95.6%到100.0%的输出通过有效性检查，但只有11.1%到19.6%能在保留无关代码的同时移除与实际变化完全相同的日志代码。代理之间存在差异，包括遗漏移除、过度移除和不相关代码编辑，且在不同移除类别和轨迹中存在显著变化。执行成本较高并不一定能提高对齐程度。提交信息和开发者讨论是最有助于对齐的因素，而使用分类指南可以一致地减少运行时间。

Conclusion: 日志移除是一项独特的软件维护任务，可靠的自动化取决于准确确定移除范围同时保留必要代码。

Abstract: Logging code supports debugging, monitoring, and software maintenance, but excessive logging can add noise, impose runtime overhead, and obscure diagnostic information. While prior research has extensively studied logging code generation and modification, logging removal remains comparatively underexplored. In this paper, we study developer logging removal practices and explore the use of coding agents for this task. We extract and manually validate logging removal cases from Python and Java repositories and derive 10 removal patterns and 11 removal reasons that characterize how and why logging code was removed in real-world software changes. We further construct LogRem, a dataset of 387 real-world cases covering direct logging statement removal, logging infrastructure removal, and logging replacement. We evaluate four coding agents with multiple model settings and compare their outputs with accepted real-world changes. Although 95.6% to 100.0% of outputs pass validity checks, only 11.1% to 19.6% remove the same logging code as the corresponding real-world change while preserving unrelated code. Agents differ through missed removals, extra removals, and unrelated code edits, with substantial variation across logging removal categories and trajectories. Execution cost varies widely, but higher cost does not consistently yield closer alignment. Commit messages and developer discussions provide the largest alignment gains, while taxonomy guidance consistently reduces runtime. Overall, our study establishes logging removal as a distinct software maintenance task and shows that reliable automation depends on accurately determining removal scope while preserving necessary code. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study to examine logging code removal from this perspective.

</details>


### [19] [Asymmetric Repository Lineage Modeling and Verifier-Guided Coordination in Concurrent AI Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04779)
*Arjun Subramanian,George Xu,Nithilan Karthik*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了多智能体编程环境中存在的协调问题，这些环境依赖于廉价信号来确定工作优先级，但只有可执行的检查器才能验证声明的属性。通过引入基于属性的验证合约和 MERGEGYM 基准（包括开时间范围预测、重放条件冲突解决和调度三个跟踪），研究人员揭示了 715 个代码对之间的冲突情况。尽管作者编写的文件集合相互独立，但仍观察到 79 个冲突（47.3%），这种不匹配符合三方合并的预期行为。通过多种机器学习模型进行分析后发现，简单的线性规则和更复杂的模型都能有效地识别冲突，而基于重放的开销分析表明，即使在现有的 Git 合并开销较低的情况下，仍需要进行彻底的验证。零样本大语言模型的性能较低，但通过结合元数据可以实现一定程度的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在解决智能体协同编程时可能出现的协调问题：廉价信号可能误导工作优先级，但实际可执行的验证器才能确保属性的正确性。研究人员设计了一个专门的验证框架和基准测试，以更深入地研究这种差异现象。

Method: 本文提出了一个基于属性的验证合约，以及 MERGEGYM 基准测试，该基准测试包含三个跟踪：开时间范围预测、重放条件冲突解决和调度。通过分析 715 个代码对的分层关系集（其中包含 167 个文本冲突）来识别冲突情况。使用一线规则、12 个特征的逻辑回归模型和未经调校的随机森林模型来预测冲突。利用零样本大语言模型（LLM）和带有元数据的 LLM 进行对比分析。

Result: - 715 个代码对中共有 79 个冲突（47.3%），这些冲突出现在独立作者编写的文件集合中，这种情况是符合三方合并的预期行为的。
- 单个线性规则 AUROC 达到 0.877，12 个特征的逻辑回归模型 AUROC 达到 0.882（95% CI: 0.845-0.917），PR-AUC 为 0.661，未经调校的随机森林模型 AUROC 达到 0.902（95% CI: 0.875-0.928），PR-AUC 为 0.701。
- 在 33.3% 的重放预算开销下，逻辑回归模型和随机森林模型分别可恢复 81.2% 和 82.9% 的冲突。
- 对于 79 个零重叠冲突中的 48 个，补丁重建成功，并使得所有冲突都变得干净；其余 31 个冲突不确定，这一检查结果证实了三方合并机制的有效性，而非发现了新的 Git 机制。
- 在跟踪 1 中，零样本 LLM 的 AUC 为 0.704，而结合元数据的 LLM 的 AUC 为 0.740。
- 在跟踪 3 中，决策时间网关在冻结标签重播情况下平均可去重 91.7% 的标签重叠冲突，但会导致 65.0% 的完成时间开销增加。
- Git 本地合并树重放的开销在日志中非常低（中位数 0.02 秒），因此研究人员不主张仅通过代码谱分类来节省检查器的开销。在开销较低的情况下，应彻底验证所有内容。

Conclusion: 研究表明，智能体协同编程时廉价信号和实际验证之间的差异是一个实际问题，提出了基于属性的验证合约和 MERGEGYM 基准测试来量化这一问题。通过机器学习模型和测试分析发现了冲突的本质和模式，揭示了三方合并的机制，并强调了在不同计算资源条件下彻底验证的重要性，尽管现有的本机重放开销较低，但仍需要进行全面验证。

Abstract: Concurrent AI coding agents create a coordination problem in which cheap signals may prioritize work, but only an executable checker can establish the property being claimed. We study this separation through a property-scoped verification contract and MERGEGYM, a three-track benchmark for open-time scope forecasting, replay- conditioned conflict resolution, and scheduling. On a stratified 715-pair lineage set (167 textual conflicts), 79 conflicts (47.3%) occur despite disjoint authored file sets. This is an operationally important proxy mismatch expected from three-way merge: authored PR diffs are measured against PR-specific bases, whereas the checker compares both heads to their common merge base. A standalone lineage-union rule reaches held-out AUROC 0.877; a 12-feature logistic model reaches 0.882 [0.845, 0.917] with PR-AUC 0.661, and an untuned random forest on the same decision-time features reaches 0.902 [0.875, 0.928] with PR-AUC 0.701. At a 33.3% held-out replay budget, the logistic and random-forest models recover 81.2% and 82.9% of conflicts, respectively. Patch reconstruction succeeds for 48/79 zero- overlap conflicts and all 48 become clean; the other 31 cases are inconclusive, so this check validates the expected three-way-merge explanation rather than claiming a new Git mechanism. In T1, a zero-shot LLM reaches AUC 0.704 and LLM-plus- metadata fusion 0.740. In T3, a decision-time gate de-overlaps a median 91.7% of labeled scope collisions at 65.0% makespan inflation under frozen-label replay. Because local git merge-tree replay is already cheap in our logs (median 0.02 s), we do not claim that lineage triage saves this checker alone: when exact replay is cheap, verify everything. All empirical guarantees in this paper remain limited to textual mergeability or the explicitly stated frozen-label scheduling target.

</details>


### [20] [Agent Skill Evolution: How Revisions Affect Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04832)
*Jiajie Wang,Yutong Zhao,Tianlin Li,Huashan Chen,Jinfu Chen,Kebin Peng,Sen He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究针对LLM编码代理的“代理技能”（SKILL.md文件）修订效果进行了大规模量化分析。通过分析数千个修订对，并在21个模型和4个代理上进行测试，发现添加可自动检查的规则（如指定命令或路径）能显著提升代理的合规性、行动率和正确性，同时评估了按需加载技能主体时的性能衰减与token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理技能（SKILL.md）的修订虽然如同代码迭代般普遍，但修订究竟如何改变代理的行为、其实际效能如何、以及在实际按需加载场景下的成本与收益，目前缺乏系统性的量化研究。

Method: 研究首先对3,159个技能的2,608对修订进行演化特征分析。接着聚焦于“规则变更”（添加/删除可自动验证的规则），在21个模型（单次回答任务）和4个代理（沙盒任务）中开展对照实验，测量合规率、行动率、最终正确性，并统计了20个模型和4个代理的token开销。

Result: 1. 55%的技能修订涉及规则或流程变更，且修订技能的提交更倾向于修改代理配置文件。2. 添加规则使单次回答合规率平均提升0.41，代理行动率提升0.23，最终正确性提升0.10。3. 规则增益主要源于指定旧技能未提及的命令或路径。4. 在实际按需加载设置下，代理保留约51%的行动增益，模型保留约38%。5. 修订增加18-19%的单次输入token，而加载技能主体使任务token增加50%。

Conclusion: 明确且具体的规则（如命令和路径）是提升代理性能的有效手段，但实际应用中按需加载机制会削弱部分增益。技能修订的性能提升需与引入的token成本（尤其是主体加载成本）进行权衡。

Abstract: Agent Skills, the SKILL.md files that tell an LLM coding agent how a project works, are revised like code, yet what a revision does to the agent is unknown. From 2,608 first/last revision pairs of 3,159 Skills, we characterize how Skills evolve and how they change together with the configuration of the agent's harness. We then focus on rule changes, revisions that add or remove a rule we can check automatically, such as "run allium check". We measure their effect on 21 models in single answers and on four agents in a sandbox, and their cost on 20 of these models and the four agents. Most revisions (55%) change a rule or procedure, and commits that revise a Skill change harness files such as CLAUDE.md more often than other commits of the same size. Across 16 open-weight models, an added rule raises compliance in a single answer by +0.41 on average. Across the four agents, the rate at which the agent takes the required action rises by +0.23 on average (+0.16 to +0.36), and for the three agents that blind judges assessed, final correctness rises by +0.10 on average (+0.06 to +0.14). The gain comes mainly from rules that name a command or path the old Skill did not mention. Real tools load a Skill's body only when the agent decides it needs it. In that setting the four agents keep about half of the action gain on average (51%), and the three open models about 38%. A revision adds 18-19% input tokens to a single answer and no detectable cost to an agent episode, while loading a Skill's body raises the tokens of an episode by 50% on average.

</details>


### [21] [AI Agent Pull Requests on GitHub: Frequency, Structure, and Merge Conflict Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04697)
*George Xu,Arjun Subramanian,Nithilan Karthik*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究首次对AI编码代理同时提交到同一仓库的Pull Request（PR）进行实证分析，发现40.2%的仓库存在协同提交，共占AI代理生成PR的79.4%，其中单代理作者构成绝大多数（99.5%），跨代理协作仅为0.5%。跨代理PR冲突率显著高于单代理（41.7% vs 19.8%），且90%置信区间不重叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对AI编码代理并发提交行为的系统性分析。随着AI工具在软件开发中的广泛应用，理解多代理同时操作同一仓库的协作模式对于预防冲突、优化工作流和保障代码质量具有重要意义。

Method: 使用AIDev-pop数据集（33,596个PR，2,807个仓库），通过精确时间重叠分析计算协同提交比例，并在一周合作窗口内重新评估。此外，对747个独特的协同PR对进行了三向git合并实验，测量文本冲突率，并基于Git报告构建冲突分类系统。

Result: 1. 40.2%的仓库包含协同提交的PR对；这些协同对占AI代理生成PR的79.4%。
2. 在一周合作窗口内，协同比例上升至53.4%和95.0%分别。
3. 大多数协同PR对是单代理作者（约99.5%），只有0.5%为跨代理协作（约4.3%仓库）。
4. 跨代理PR的文本冲突率显著高于单代理（41.7% vs 19.8%），90%置信区间不重叠。
5. 冲突主要发生在源代码文件中（84.4%），而非依赖清单；约42%为结构性冲突（修改/删除或添加/添加）。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了AI编码代理并发提交的关键特征：单代理协作主导，跨代理协作罕见但冲突风险极高。研究强调需要更智能的冲突检测与解决机制，以应对AI代理在同一仓库内的密集交互场景。

Abstract: AI coding agents may generate and submit Pull Requests (PRs) to the same repository at the same time. However, research concerning the extent of concurrent submission by AI coding agents to a common repository does not exist. This paper uses the AIDev-pop dataset (33,596 PRs in 2,807 repositories) to provide the first empirical examination of the prevalence of concurrent submission using PRs authored by agents. We report that when considering exact temporal overlap, 40.2% of repositories contain co-active agent-authored PR pairs; further, the co-active pairs account for 79.4% of all PRs generated by an AI agent. When we examine co-activity within a one week collaboration window, the percentages are increased to 53.4% and 95.0%, respectively. For the majority of the co-active PR pairs (underlying the vast majority of which are intra-agent authored), both PRs were authored by the same agent, while only 0.5% of co-active pairs were cross-agent, and occurred in only 122 out of 2807 total repositories examined (or approximately 4.3%). Additionally, we replayed actual three way git merges on 747 unique co-active pairs (one per repository), and computed the percentage of textual conflict encountered during the merge operation to combine the two PRs in each pair. We observed that the percentage of textual conflict encountered was significantly higher for cross-agent pairs compared to intra-agent pairs: 41.7% vs. 19.8%, respectively, with non-overlapping 95% confidence intervals. Lastly, we developed a classification system based on the detection of conflict reported by git, and determined that the majority of conflicts resulted from modifications to source code files (84.4% of conflicted files) and not dependency manifest files; further, nearly 42% of conflicts we observed were structural (i.e., modify/delete or add/add).

</details>


### [22] [CARET: Training-Free Test-Time Scaling for Repository-Level Code Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04837)
*Jiajie Wang,Yutong Zhao,Kebin Peng,Sen He,Qing Guo,Tianlin Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出训练无感方法 CARET，联合优化仓库级代码补全的检索与生成阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 工作侧重检索而忽视生成，认为生成阶段可通过测试时扩展获益，两者协同改进优于单一优化。

Method: 检索阶段依据样本一致性路由上下文，分歧时切换备用上下文；生成阶段缓存提示前缀避免重复编码，利用逆向上下文似然（模型自我评分后续代码概率）筛选最终补全。

Result: 在三个基准和 6 个模型（1.1B-7B）的 18 种组合中，精确匹配平均提升 10.8（vs 贪婪解码）和 5.3（vs self-consistency@10），计算开销约 1.45 倍单次生成。

Conclusion: 协同改进检索与生成能显著提升代码补全准确率，同时保持接近单次通过的预算成本。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) dominates repository-level code completion: it retrieves cross-file context (R), then decodes one greedy completion (G). Existing work mainly focuses on retrieval and stops there. We argue both stages can be improved together, with generation in particular gaining from test-time scaling. We present CARET, a training-free method. For R, CARET routes among retrieval contexts using the agreement among its own samples, cascading to an alternative context when the samples scatter. For G, it samples candidates over a cached prompt prefix, so the long retrieved context is encoded once rather than once per sample. It then selects the final completion by reverse-context likelihood: a correct completion makes the code after the cursor more probable, so the same model grades its own candidates by reading ahead. Across CrossCodeEval, RepoEval-Line, and RepoEval-API with six code models (1.1B to 7B, four families), CARET improves exact match in all 18 combinations by 10.8 points on average over greedy decoding and 5.3 over self-consistency@10. Token-level compute stays near 1.45 times one generation (measured wall-clock 1.3-2.9 times, growing with generator size). Improving retrieval and generation together yields more accurate code than improving retrieval alone, at a budget that stays close to a single pass.

</details>


### [23] [Complex Agents, Shallow Tests: Demystifying and Enhancing Test Adequacy of Agent Harness in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04921)
*Yifan Xiong,Jingyi Ge,Zhenpeng Chen,Yiling Lou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对 LLM 智能体系统中 harness 测试不足的问题，首次进行了实证研究，并提出 HarnessTester 测试生成工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体 harness 复杂性增加，实现缺陷引发可靠性担忧，且现有测试对关键处理 LLM 输出的 LDH 代码覆盖严重不足。

Method: 开展首次实证研究调查真实系统 harness 测试充分性，并提出 HarnessTester，利用显式 agent-harness 契约构建符合契约的测试设置以充分练习 LDH 代码。

Result: HarnessTester 在线/分支覆盖率上超越最先进通用技术 75.95%/84.76%，变异分数提升 69.89%，并在 OpenClaw 等系统中发现 122 个真实缺陷（88 个未知，69 个被开发者确认）。

Conclusion: HarnessTester 能有效提高真实智能体系统的测试充分性与可靠性，具有显著的实践有效性。

Abstract: LLM-based agentic systems are emerging as a new software paradigm. Modern agents are typically composed of backbone LLMs and a surrounding harness that serves as the operational software infrastructure for agent execution. As agent harnesses grow increasingly complex, agents suffer from diverse harness implementation bugs, raising substantial reliability concerns. In this work, we conduct the first empirical study to systematically investigate the test adequacy of harness in real-world agentic systems. Our analysis reveals that agent harness remains substantially undertested. In particular, LLM-dependent harness (LDH) code, despite its critical role in processing LLM outputs and governing agent behavior, receives limited testing attention, with less than half of its lines and branches covered by existing tests. Motivated by these findings, we further propose HarnessTester, the first harness-oriented test generation technique that incorporates explicit agent-harness contract support to construct contract-faithful test setups and extensively exercise LDH code. Our evaluation shows that HarnessTester substantially outperforms state-of-the-art general-purpose test generation techniques in achieving 75.95%/84.76% larger line/branch coverage gains and 69.89% larger mutation-score gains. Furthermore, HarnessTester detects 122 real-world harness bugs in widely-used agentic systems (e.g., OpenClaw), among which, 88 bugs are previously-unknown bugs and 69 bugs have been confirmed by agent developers. These results highlight the practical effectiveness of HarnessTester in improving test adequacy and assuring the reliability of real-world agentic systems.

</details>


### [24] [Assembling Insights for Agentic Machine Learning Engineering Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04927)
*Bihui Jin,Yinxi Li,Kaiyuan Wang,Pengyu Nie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基准测试(MLE-InsightBench)和一个智能体(MLE-InsightForge),用于系统性研究来自相关MLE任务的'洞见'(insights)对自动化机器学习工程(agentic MLE)系统的影响。研究发现,将160个Kaggle竞赛中的洞见注入后,私有测试分数提升了123.9%,且不同类型的洞见是互补的,不同的注入方式(skills vs playbook)在不同任务上各有优劣,优势在于更具成本效益,且提升效果在不同后端LLM上通用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic machine learning engineering(MLE)是一种新兴的AI4SE应用,旨在处理复杂的ML任务,并是AI系统递归自我改进的一种途径。近期的agentic MLE系统表明,利用相关MLE任务的洞见非常有价值,但洞见的收集和注入方式仍然是零散的(ad hoc),且现有的评估往往无法控制洞见的来源,导致数据泄露成为有效性的威胁。为了弥补这些空缺,本文系统性地研究了来自动相关MLE任务的洞见如何影响agentic MLE系统,以及其影响如何取决于洞见类型和注入方法。

Method: 作者引入了MLE-InsightBench,一个包含160个Kaggle竞赛、涵盖12个领域的基准测试。为防止数据泄露,每个领域中有一个竞赛被保留作为目标任务,而其他148个竞赛仅作为来源,且需确保这些来源不存在受目标任务影响的可能性(如通过后续版本或重复使用解决方案)。他们还开发了MLE-InsightForge,一个洞见组装智能体,它探索来源竞赛、顶尖人类解决方案和写作,构建三种洞见类型:12个领域洞见(总结最佳实践),148个竞赛洞见(描述获胜解决方案),以及16个改进洞见(捕捉通用的调试和优化策略)。这些洞见以两种方式注入:即用型技能(on-demand skills)或全面的策略(playbook)。

Result: 在评估中,提取的洞见将归一化私有测试分数提升了123.9%,相比于没有洞见的基线。受控实验进一步表明:1)三种类型的洞见是互补的;2)更好的注入模式(skills vs playbook)因任务而异,但总体而言技能更具成本效益;3)这些收益在不同的后端LLM上具有普适性。

Conclusion: 本研究系统性地量化了相关MLE任务洞见对agentic MLE系统性能的显著影响,并通过严格的基准测试设计有效控制了数据泄露的风险。结果表明,结构化的洞见收集与注入能够显著提升性能,且具备跨LLM的泛化能力,为未来agentic MLE系统在相关任务间共享知识提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Agentic machine learning engineering (MLE) is an emerging AI4SE application for complex ML tasks and a step toward recursive self-improvement of AI systems. Recent agentic MLE systems show the value of leveraging insights from related MLE tasks: some systems condition code generation on expert domain knowledge, which is implicitly curated from peer MLE tasks; some systems have a loop of solving an MLE task, gathering memory to benefit future tasks. However, insight collection and injection remain ad hoc, and existing evaluations often do not control insight sources, making data leakage a threat to validity.
  To close these gaps, we systematically study how insights from related MLE tasks affect agentic MLE systems, and how their impact depends on insight type and injection method. We introduce MLE-InsightBench, a benchmark of 160 Kaggle competitions across 12 domains. To prevent leakage, one competition per domain is held out as the target, while the remaining 148 serve as sources only when they cannot have been influenced by the target task, such as through later editions or reused solutions. We also develop MLE-InsightForge, an insight assembly agent that explores source competitions, top human solutions, and writeups to construct three insight types: 12 domain insights summarizing best practices, 148 competition insights describing winning solutions, and 16 improvement insights capturing generic debugging and optimization strategies. These insights are injected either as on-demand skills or as an upfront playbook. In our evaluation, the extracted insights improve the normalized private-test score by 123.9% over a no-insight baseline. Controlled experiments further show that the three insight types are complementary; the better injection mode (skill vs. playbook) varies by tasks, with skills more cost-effective overall; and the gains generalize across backend LLMs.

</details>


### [25] [TeleGen: Improving LLM-Based Web Application Generation via Runtime Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04981)
*Yujia Luo,Haonan Zhang,Jiasi Shen,Zishuo Ding,Weiyi Shang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TeleGen通过在生成的Web应用中植入观测点，收集运行时遥测数据并压缩为简要报告，帮助LLM修复代码，提高任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成-执行-修复流水线只能利用任务结果或错误信息，缺少运行时细节，导致交互层面的失败难以定位。

Method: TeleGen在生成的应用程序中插桩以捕获浏览器交互、网络请求、DOM变化等运行时遥测；将原始遥测日志压缩成简短摘要（brief）作为修复提示；在LLM生成后执行、收集遥测、生成brief、喂给LLM进行修复。

Result: 在WebGen-Bench上，成功率从67.7%提升至76.2%（+8.5pp）；在Web-Bench上，累计Pass@2从21.7%提升至29.8%；消融表明遥测提供有用诊断信号，brief使其更有效且成本更低；尤其对隐藏执行路径（导航、表单流程、前后端协同）的失败帮助显著。

Conclusion: 运行时观测增强能够弥合生成代码与任务执行之间的信息鸿沟，显著提升LLM生成Web应用的可靠性，为未来的代码生成与自动修复提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Large language models can generate runnable web applications from natural-language requirements, but many generated applications still fail interactive tasks. Existing generate-execute-repair pipelines execute the generated application and use task outcomes or error messages to guide code revision. However, this feedback often misses the runtime behavior between a browser action and the final task outcome, making interaction-level failures difficult to diagnose. Therefore, we propose TeleGen, an observability-enhanced framework for LLM-based web application generation. TeleGen instruments generated applications, collects runtime telemetry during task execution, and compresses raw telemetry logs into concise briefs for repair. We evaluate TeleGen on WebGen-Bench and Web-Bench. On WebGen-Bench, TeleGen improves task success from 67.7% with repair without telemetry to 76.2%, an increase of 8.5 percentage points. On Web-Bench, it improves cumulative Pass@2 from 21.7% to 29.8%. Ablation results show that runtime telemetry provides a useful diagnostic signal, while telemetry briefs make this signal more effective and less costly to use. Further analysis shows that telemetry is especially helpful for failures involving hidden execution paths, such as navigation, form workflows, and frontend-backend coordination.

</details>


### [26] [LLM-Based Test Generation: Information Sources, Generation Strategies, and Quality Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05001)
*Yunhao Liang,Chengguang Gan,Ruixuan Ying,Hanjun Wei,Zhe Cui,Shiwen Ni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对大型语言模型（LLM）在生成测试场景、可执行测试套件、断言、交互序列和模糊测试基础设施方面的应用进行了结构化叙述性综述。通过整理95篇精选文献，从测试目标与产物、训练与生成信息、生成机制和评估证据四个维度构建了一个系统性框架，用以连接测试生成过程与用于证明测试质量的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型越来越多地用于生成各种测试产物，但这些产物服务于不同的测试目的（如提高覆盖率、与参考程序一致、检测需求违规），且依赖于不同的正确行为信息源。研究动机在于将测试生成过程与用于证明测试质量的特定证据类型系统地连接起来，以明确不同测试 artifact 的独特价值。

Method: 采用结构化叙述性综述方法，整理了截至2026年9月29日的95篇精选文献。研究沿四个维度组织领域：测试目标与产物、训练与生成时的可用信息、生成与学习机制、以及评估证据。综合了单元测试、基于需求的测试、测试预言（oracle）、API和GUI测试、模糊测试以及学习型测试生成器的研究。

Result: 研究构建了一个综合框架，阐明了执行反馈如何提高可执行性同时影响预言（oracle）的独立性，规范来源和训练时参考监督在对比中的重要性，以及下游代码选择增益与测试正确性之间的差异。此外，该研究还对基准测试进行了系统组织，并揭示了当前存在的差距。

Conclusion: 结论部分利用这些区别对基准测试进行了系统组织，并提出了未来的研究议程，重点包括：独立预言评估、预算感知的生成策略、仓库级（repository-scale）评估以及测试维护。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to generate test scenarios, executable test suites, assertions, interaction sequences, and fuzzing infrastructure. These artifacts serve different purposes and rely on different sources of information about correct behavior. A test that increases implementation coverage, a test that agrees with a reference program, and a test that detects a requirements violation therefore provide distinct kinds of evidence. We present a structured narrative survey that connects the generation process to the evidence used to justify test quality. Drawing on 95 curated source records through 29 September 2026, we organize the field along four dimensions: testing objectives and artifacts, information available during training and generation, generation and learning mechanisms, and evaluation evidence. We synthesize work on unit and requirements-based testing, test oracles, API and GUI testing, fuzzing, and learned test generators. The resulting framework explains how execution feedback can improve executability while also affecting the independence of an oracle, why specification provenance and training-time reference supervision matter for comparisons, and how downstream code-selection gains differ from test correctness. We use these distinctions to organize benchmarks and derive a research agenda for independent oracle assessment, budget-aware generation, repository-scale evaluation, and test maintenance.

</details>


### [27] [Rethinking Tool Design for Agentic RCA: A Controlled Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05009)
*Yu Luo,Rongchen Gao,Zhenhui Zhou,Changchang Liu,Yuliang You,Yongqian Sun,Shenglin Zhang,Qiuai Fu,Shijie Wang,Dan Pei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过控制实证方法探索了大型语言模型在微服务根因分析中的工具抽象和组合。研究者实施了24个分层工具（L0–L3）和一个Python参考环境，使用375个微服务故障案例评估了Qwen3.7-Plus的8种配置并比较了4个Qwen模型的效果。结果表明：(1) L3在top‑1定位准确率上略低于L0，但每例耗时减少超过一半；(2) 增加工具层级可改善故障类型识别，但会降低定位准确率；(3) 轨迹分析揭示了某些情况下代理在查询额外证据后会推翻正确的诊断建议；(4) 不同模型对工具级别的反应各异，例如L3对L1+L2的补充对三个模型均有利，但会损害Qwen‑8 B的性能（该模型很少调用L3）；(5) 工具配置的排名随微服务系统的不同而变化。该研究为根据模型、诊断目标和目标系统来设计和选择工具提供了实证依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型代理在微服务根因分析中的应用日益受到关注，但关于如何设计和组合工具的实证指导仍较为有限。现有研究缺乏对工具抽象、组合及其对诊断效果影响的系统性评估。

Method: 本研究实施了24个分层结构化工具（L1：指标访问，L2：证据分析，L3：诊断），并设计了一个Python参考环境（L0）。研究者收集了来自三个微服务系统的375个故障案例，利用Qwen3.7-Plus评估了8种工具配置，并使用相同的4个配置比较了4个Qwen模型。分析方法包括准确率计算、时间消耗比较、轨迹追踪以及跨模型和系统的性能比较。

Result: 主要实验结果包括：
- Qwen3.7-Plus下，L3的根因定位top‑1准确率为82.8%，低于L0的85.4%，但每例平均处理时间减少了超过一半。
- 添加工具层级可提升故障类型识别准确率，却会降低定位准确率。
- 轨迹分析显示，在某些案例中，代理在咨询额外证据后会推翻原本正确的诊断建议。
- 不同模型对工具级别的反应各异：L3对L1+L2的补充对三个模型均有效，但会损害Qwen-8B的性能（该模型很少调用L3）。
- 工具配置的排名随微服务系统的不同而变化。

Conclusion: 该实证研究为设计和使用根因分析工具提供了坚实的依据。通过揭示工具配置对不同模型和微服务系统的差异化影响，研究结果可指导用户根据特定的语言模型、诊断目标和目标系统来选择和组合工具，从而实现更高效的根因分析。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly explored for root cause analysis (RCA) in microservice systems, yet empirical guidance on how to design and combine their tools remains limited. We conduct a controlled empirical study of tool abstraction and composition across models and microservice environments. We implement 24 structured tools for metric access (L1), evidence analysis (L2), and diagnosis (L3), alongside a Python-based reference setting (L0). Using 375 failure cases from three microservice systems, we evaluate eight configurations with Qwen3.7-Plus and compare four Qwen models on a shared subset of four configurations. Our results show that tool configurations affect root cause localization and failure type identification differently. With Qwen3.7-Plus, L3 achieves 82.8% top-1 localization accuracy compared with 85.4% for L0, while requiring less than half the time per case. Adding tool levels can improve type identification while reducing localization accuracy. Trajectory analysis reveals cases in which agents override correct diagnostic recommendations after consulting additional evidence. Model choice also changes tool benefits: adding L3 to L1+L2 improves diagnosis for three models but hurts Qwen3-8B, which rarely invokes L3. Tool configuration rankings further change across microservice systems. These findings provide an empirical foundation for designing and using RCA tools, guiding tool selection and composition according to the model, diagnostic objective, and target system.

</details>


### [28] [Characterizing Security Effects of OSS Vulnerabilities in Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05036)
*Yu Ji,Yang Wei,Yutao Hu,Haojun Zhao,Yueming Wu,Deqing Zou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究开源软件漏洞在代理工作流中的传播机制，提出基于漏洞感知语义信息、易受/修复版本的差分执行以及跨层运行时来源的方法，以判断漏洞是否产生可观察的安全效应并确定其影响的最远层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理越来越依赖开源组件，这些依赖的安全缺陷可能通过工具输出、代理状态和模型暴露传播，难以判断其实际后果以及影响在系统中的边界。

Method: 收集已知OSS漏洞，在代理工作流中执行易受和修复版本，利用漏洞感知的语义信息进行差分比较，结合多层运行时来源追踪，检测可观察的安全效应并定位其传播至的最高层。

Result: 所提出的方法能够准地区分已实现的漏洞效应并在多种漏洞场景中确定其表现边界；在真实代理框架的文档化工作流中应用，发现多个由已知OSS依赖漏洞引起的安全效应。

Conclusion: 评估开源依赖的风险应关注其执行层面的实际后果而不仅仅是漏洞的存在，这凸显了在代理系统中进行漏洞影响分析的必要性。

Abstract: Software agents increasingly depend on open-source components when executing tools and interacting with external systems. Security flaws in these dependencies may therefore influence more than the software process in which they occur: their consequences can be carried through tool outputs, agent state, and information subsequently exposed to the model. Determining whether such a consequence is actually realized in a particular execution, and where its influence stops within the agent system, remains challenging.
  We investigate how known OSS vulnerabilities behave when exercised as part of agent workflows. Our study reveals recurring patterns in the way security-relevant consequences emerge and propagate across runtime layers. Building on these observations, we use vulnerability-aware semantic information, differential executions of vulnerable and corrected software, and runtime provenance spanning multiple layers to determine whether a vulnerability produces an observable security effect and to identify the furthest layer at which that effect remains manifested.
  Our evaluation shows that this approach can accurately distinguish realized vulnerability effects and determine their manifestation boundaries across a diverse collection of vulnerability scenarios. We additionally apply the analysis to documented workflows in a real-world agent framework and uncover multiple security effects originating from known vulnerabilities in its OSS dependencies. These results highlight the importance of reasoning about vulnerable dependencies in terms of their execution-level consequences rather than vulnerability presence alone.

</details>


### [29] [Beyond Task Completion: Measuring Interaction Cost in Terminal User Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05047)
*Ruida Hu,Yuanhao Wang,Chao Peng,Yakun Zhang,Cuiyun Gao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出“智能体即用户”评估范式，利用 LLM 代理量化终端用户界面（TUI）的可用性与交互成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务完成度不足以反映界面理解难度；现有评估缺乏基于已验证执行的重复性测量。

Method: 通过 Agent-KLM 分离解释与操作成本，TUINaut 记录轨迹并用预言机验证；实验覆盖 84 任务、15 个真实 TUI 及 45 个生成界面。

Result: 成功率掩盖了人与智能体任务差异；操作成本排名与任务难度高度相关；功能正确的生成界面仍需可用性优化。

Conclusion: 该范式可作为可重复、基于执行的测量工具，用于评估任务型可用性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used through terminal user interfaces (TUIs), yet task completion alone does not capture how difficult an interface is to understand and operate. Existing human assessments and LLM-generated ratings or reports do not provide repeatable measurements of interaction effort grounded in verified task execution.
  We propose Agent-as-a-User, an evaluation paradigm that places an LLM agent in the user role. Agent-KLM separates agent-side interpretation and operation costs and relates them structurally to human interaction. TUINaut operationalizes the paradigm by recording interaction trajectories and verifying outcomes with actor-independent oracles.
  Using TUINaut-Bench, we study 84 tasks across 15 real-world TUIs with six human participants and five observation-evaluator configurations, and evaluate 45 TUIs generated by three leading stacks. Similar aggregate success rates mask differences in which tasks humans and agents complete, whereas interpretation and operation costs follow correlated task rankings, especially for operation. This pattern persists across configurations. Generated TUIs can implement correct functionality while still requiring usability improvements. These results position Agent-as-a-User as a repeatable, execution-grounded paradigm for measuring task-based usability.

</details>


### [30] [SpecAgent: Empowering Program Verification with Agentic Synthesis of Formal Program Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05132)
*Lezhi Ma,Han Wang,Shangqing Liu,Jiawan Wang,Lei Bu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了SpecAgent，一个用于为真实世界C程序自动合成高质量ACSL形式化规范的智能体框架，旨在解决复杂依赖导致的级联验证失败问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动构建形式化规范极其耗时。现有基于大语言模型（LLM）的方法多采用仅前向的工作流和局部修复，面对具有复杂函数和循环依赖的真实程序时，先前生成的规范出错会导致级联失败，而局部修复无法解决此类全局性依赖问题。

Method: SpecAgent集成了四个核心组件：依赖感知规划（识别规范目标）、检索增强生成（RAG，提供规范模式和上下文）、智能体修复（诊断缺陷并回溯修正依赖规范）以及智能体批判（评估并精炼语义强度）。

Result: 在50个真实世界程序（来自14个仓库）和811个验证目标上评估：结合DeepSeek-V4，SpecAgent实现了96.82%的精确率和88.95%的召回率，并成功完成了604个验证目标，所有指标均显著超越现有基线。

Conclusion: SpecAgent通过引入依赖感知和多智能体协作机制，能够有效合成高质量、强语义的规范，显著提升了真实世界C程序的自动验证效率和成功率。

Abstract: Formal specifications are essential for deductive program verification, providing semantic abstractions for compositional verification of complex software. However, manually constructing specifications is labor-intensive, motivating automated synthesis. Despite recent advances in large language models (LLMs), existing approaches often rely on forward-only workflows and localized repair, limiting their effectiveness on real-world programs with complex dependencies among functions and loops. Verification failures may stem from previously generated specifications, causing cascading failures that local refinement cannot resolve. To address these challenges, we present SpecAgent, an agentic framework for synthesizing high-quality ACSL specifications for real-world C programs. SpecAgent integrates four components: dependency-aware planning to identify specification targets, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) to provide relevant specification patterns and program context, agentic repair to diagnose defects and revisit dependent specifications, and agentic critique to assess and refine semantic strength beyond proof success. We evaluate SpecAgent on specification synthesis and program verification tasks. On 50 programs from 14 real-world repositories, SpecAgent with DeepSeek-V4 achieves 96.82% precision and 88.95% recall in synthesizing correct and strong specifications, outperforming all baselines. On 811 real-world verification targets, it successfully discharges 604, also surpassing existing baselines. These results demonstrate SpecAgent's effectiveness in synthesizing high-quality specifications and facilitating real-world program verification.

</details>


### [31] [StateWise: Diagnosing and Repairing Persistent Operational State Before Agent Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05241)
*Yongyuan Peng,Zhou Feng,Tongying Wu,Jiahao Chen,Yuan Su,Chunyi Zhou,Tianyu Du,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: StateWise 框架通过检测和修复持久操作状态中的失效记录，使得大模型编码代理在环境变化后仍能正确执行任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大模型代理依赖持久化的操作记录作为后续行动的前提，但环境或需求的变化会使这些记录失效，现有的动作审计、溯源和澄清机制无法纠正底层状态，导致任务失败甚至不安全的修改。

Method: StateWise 首先使用记录层面的反事实重新规划定位决策关键记录，接着通过可靠性检查、只读验证机器可观察事实以及对开发者意图的针对性澄清来判断记录的当前有效性；采用类型化证据将证据绑定到具体记录和范围，实现带修复血统的持久校正；修复后代理从修正状态重新规划，并执行独立的状态‑动作一致性检查后再进行实际操作。

Result: 在涵盖多种运行时环境、工作区配置和仓库设置的 150 个可执行编码代理案例上的评估表明，在持久状态被损坏的情况下，StateWise 达到 93.3% 的整体正确率，而基线代理仅为 38.7%，且未产生任何不安全操作；消融实验、多任务实验和迁移评估进一步验证了其恢复效果、持久校正以及跨仓库和工具接口的可迁移性。

Conclusion: StateWise 提供了一种可靠的手段来在行动执行前诊断和修复持久操作状态，显著提升大模型编码代理的正确性和安全性，使其能够在动态且多变的环境中稳定工作。

Abstract: LLM agents combine reasoning, tool use, and persistent memory to support work across tasks by reusing stored operational records as premises for later actions. However, environmental or requirement changes can invalidate these records, while existing action review, provenance tracking, and clarification mechanisms may leave the underlying persistent state uncorrected. Our audit of coding-agent trajectories identifies candidate failure chains in which invalid records are reused, leading to task failures and unsafe modifications. We propose StateWise, a framework for diagnosing and repairing persistent operational state before action execution. StateWise uses record-level counterfactual replanning to identify decision-critical records, then establishes their current validity through reliability checks, read-only verification of machine-observable facts, and targeted clarification of developer-owned intent. Typed evidence grounding binds evidence to specific records and scopes, enabling persistent corrections with repair lineage. The agent then replans from the repaired state, followed by an independent state-action check before execution. We evaluate StateWise on 150 executable coding-agent cases across diverse runtime environments, workspace configurations, and repository settings, complemented by cross-model evaluations. Under corrupted persistent state, StateWise achieves 93.3% overall correctness, compared with 38.7% for the baseline agent, with no unsafe actions. Component ablations, multi-task experiments, and transfer evaluations further demonstrate effective recovery, persistent corrections, and transferability across repositories and tool interfaces.

</details>
