Extensive simulations in dense urban environments demonstrate that under strict infrastructure budget constraints, the proposed GNN-DRL framework consistently outperforms greedy heuristic baselines by up to ~12% in coverage while delivering faster inference speed via GPU acceleration.

</details>


### [85] [SoK: Semantic Decision Engines in Network Control Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06425)
*Delong Li,Chen Li,Xu Wang,Haochen Gong,Rui Lang,Guangsheng Yu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 研究发现，尽管许多语义决策引擎声称能在控制环路或时间预算内工作，但只有少数给出匹配的测量支持，且在缺乏确定性计算步骤的情况下，几乎所有引擎都无法保证满足期限，且在排队场景下更易失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了澄清语义决策引擎在实时控制环路中的实际可靠性，作者系统化了139篇论文族的决策接口、执行路径和检查归属，暴露出声称与实际测量之间的巨大差距。

Method: 对139个论文族进行分类，统计宣称适用控制环路或时间预算的数量，检验是否配有匹配测量；针对缺乏确定性计算步骤的72族进行有界测试，观察其对入场裁决的影响；比较单请求与排队场景下的时延表现及覆盖检查的一致性。

Result: 只有50族声称符合控制环路/时间预算，其中仅4族提供匹配测量；全体139族中仅4族报告期限达成；在无确定性计算步骤的72族中，它们贡献了22项声称但均无支持，且仅有2族明确覆盖责任人；有界测试表明每个差距可反转入场裁决，且引擎在孤立请求下满足10 s预算而在排队后失效，同一引擎在不同覆盖检查上表现不一致。

Conclusion: 作者提出最小报告记录、设计规则以及研究议程，以帮助决策引擎真正纳入控制环路，确保时限满足和可验证性。

Abstract: A semantic decision engine such as Jev can return a valid answer and still miss a network deadline, select an infeasible action or leave the service unverified. We systematize 139 paper families by decision interface, execution path and check ownership. Fifty families claim that their engine fits a control loop or time budget, but only four support the claim with matched measurement. Across all 139, four report deadline attainment. The gap concentrates where the decision has no deterministic computation step. Those 72 families make 22 of the claims, none supported, and name a coverage owner in only two. Bounded tests under one event model show that each gap can reverse an admission verdict. A decision that meets a 10 s budget for every isolated request meets it for none once decisions queue ahead of replayed execution times. The same engine passes one coverage check and fails another. We derive a minimum reporting record, design rules and a research agenda for admitting decision engines to control loops.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [86] [BitIR: Cross-Architecture Fault Injection for Resilience Analysis of Heterogeneous GPU Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04037)
*Maisy Dunlavy,Michael Papka,Zhiling Lan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该工作提出跨架构故障注入框架BitIR，在LLVM IR层面注入确定性单比特故障，以便在NVIDIA、Intel和AMD GPU上进行语义等价的比较，揭示不同供应商GPU对相同故障的行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有韧性研究多局限于单一架构，缺乏对不同GPU供应商之间故障传播行为的了解，因而需要一个能够在异构平台上进行公平比较的跨架构故障注入手段。

Method: 在LLVM IR阶段插入确定性单比特故障，生成经编译器后端降级前仍保持语义等价的可执行文件，分别在ALCF的Polaris（NVIDIA A100）、ALCF的Aurora（Intel GPU Max 1550）和OLCF的Frontier（AMD Instinct MI250X）三台领先级超算上，对代表性异构基准进行大规模故障注入，并将结果划分为掩码、沉默数据错误（SDC）和硬失败三类。

Result: 相同故障在不同供应商上表现截然不同：Intel GPU大多被掩码但逃逸后易产生挂起；NVIDIA GPU更容易暴露出可检测的硬失败；AMD GPU则依据基准和故障位置在SDC主导和失败主导之间交替。这些结果表明韧性与后端和工作负载强相关。

Conclusion: 韧性不是后端不变的属性，故障缓解策略必须考虑具体的GPU后端和运行的工作负载，才能在异构HPC系统中实现有效的容错。

Abstract: Modern GPU-based HPC systems rely on heterogeneous vendor stacks, yet resilience studies are largely limited to single architectures, leaving it unclear how faults behave across different GPU backends. We present \emph{BitIR}, a cross-architecture fault injection framework that injects deterministic single-bit faults at the LLVM IR level, ensuring semantically equivalent perturbations prior to backend lowering and enabling direct cross-vendor comparison across NVIDIA, Intel, and AMD GPUs. We evaluate BitIR on three production supercomputers -- Polaris (ALCF, NVIDIA A100), Aurora (ALCF, Intel GPU Max 1550), and Frontier (OLCF, AMD Instinct MI250X) -- representing the full spectrum of current leadership-class GPU architectures. Using representative heterogeneous benchmarks, we conduct large-scale injection campaigns across all three systems and classify outcomes into masked results, silent data corruptions (SDCs), and failures. Our results show that identical faults produce markedly different behaviors across vendors: Intel most often masks faults but exhibits more hangs when faults escape masking, NVIDIA exposes more detectable hard failures, and AMD alternates between SDC-dominant and failure-dominant behavior depending on the benchmark and fault site. These findings demonstrate that resilience is not backend-invariant, underscoring the need for backend-aware and workload-aware fault mitigation.

</details>


### [87] [OACM: Optimistic Asynchronous Communication Model for Large-Scale SNN Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04236)
*Guangnan Feng,Yangle Zeng,Honghui Liang,Zhiguang Chen,Yutong Lu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: OACM 是一种乐观异步通信模型，通过混合时间驱动与乐观回滚、单边无握手通信以及自适应消息聚合与路由，显著提升大规模脉冲神经网络仿真的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间驱动仿真器因频繁同步导致开销大，乐观PDES方法又产生大量二次回滚，通信模式低效，导致网络带宽未被充分利用，阻碍大规模分布式SNN仿真的可伸缩性。

Method: 提出三项关键创新：1）乐观混合SNN仿真，保持时间驱动的实现简单性，通过在同步窗口内的策略性回滚降低同步频率；2）基于UNR库的单边异步通信，消除握手延迟，实现完全的计算-通信重叠；3）在2D-HyperX虚拟拓扑中采用自适应消息聚合与路由策略，优化小消息流量的带宽利用率。

Result: 在高性能计算集群上，使用多区域狨猴脑模型（最高174计算节点）进行评估，OACM相较于原始CORTEX仿真器实现最高1.4倍加速，相比NEST实现超过22.6倍加速。

Conclusion: OACM 有效缓解了同步开销、回滚成本和通信低效等瓶颈，展示了在大规模分布式环境中实现高效、可伸缩的脉冲神经网络仿真的可行性。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Network (SNN) simulation serves as a crucial tool for understanding brain dynamics and advancing neuromorphic computing, but faces significant scalability challenges in large-scale distributed implementations. The primary bottlenecks arise from frequent synchronization overhead in time-driven simulators and extensive secondary rollbacks in optimistic PDES approaches, compounded by inefficient communication patterns that underutilize network bandwidth. In this paper, we propose OACM, an Optimistic Asynchronous Communication Model that addresses these challenges through three key innovations. First, we design Optimistic Hybrid SNN Simulation that combines the implementation simplicity of time-driven approaches with reduced synchronization frequency through strategic rollback mechanisms within synchronization windows. Second, we implement asynchronous one-sided communication using the UNR library, eliminating handshake latency and achieving complete computation-communication overlap. Third, we develop adaptive message aggregation and routing strategies within a 2D-HyperX virtual topology to optimize bandwidth utilization for small message traffic. Experimental evaluation on a high-performance computing cluster demonstrates that OACM achieves up to 1.4x speedup over the original CORTEX simulator and over 22.6x speedup compared to NEST when simulating a multi-area marmoset brain model at scales of up to 174 compute nodes.

</details>


### [88] [SyclKittens: A Tile Programming Model for Programmers and Coding Agents on Intel GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04277)
*Yehong Jiang,Sheng Chen,Fangwen Fu,Yen-Kuang Chen,Xinmin Tian,Stuart H. Sul,Simran Arora*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AI coding agents can write kernels but need hardware-aware interfaces; SyclKittens, a tile programming model that encodes Intel GPU best practices, lets agents achieve 82% of tuned kernel performance and a suite reaches ~96% of oneDNN, speeding Llama-3.1-8B inference 1.59-2.91x.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current AI accelerators require expert-tuned kernels for peak performance, and AI agents need better interfaces to translate hardware capabilities into efficient code.

Method: Controlled experiments on Intel GPUs using three coding models and three kernels, comparing raw SYCL/execution feedback with SyclKittens, a hardware-aware tile programming model that encodes known-good methods.

Result: SyclKittens raised kernel performance from 57.5% to 82.1% of Intel's tuned oneDNN library; a kernel suite achieved ~96% geometric mean and Llama-3.1-8B inference ran 1.59x faster than torch.compile and up to 2.91x faster than a matched multi-GPU decode path.

Conclusion: Encoding hardware-aware programming methods enables AI agents to approach hand-tuned performance, showing that proper interfaces are essential for efficient kernel development on new accelerators.

Abstract: New AI accelerators arrive before the kernels that make them fast, because peak kernel performance requires architecture-specific expertise in operand pipelines and data-movement techniques. Coding agents can now write, compile, and tune kernels on their own, so they could greatly accelerate kernel development and optimization. What agents produce depends on the interfaces they are given. These interfaces may expose a machine's raw capabilities or encode the known-good methods for exploiting its hardware features efficiently. We test the performance impact of these two interfaces on Intel GPUs through controlled experiments with three coding models on three kernels. With raw SYCL and execution feedback, Opus 4.8, the strongest of the three models tested, writes a single-GPU GEMM kernel reaching only 57.5% of Intel's tuned oneDNN library. We present SyclKittens, a hardware-aware tile programming model for Intel GPUs that encodes these known-good methods, so its operations place operands in matrix-engine layouts, prefetch through the L1 cache, and communicate over the fabric that links GPU stacks into a node. With SyclKittens under the same agent, task, and feedback budget, the agent reaches 82.1%, showing that encoded methods turn hardware capabilities into performance. Workload-specific policies stay programmable, and engineers and agents jointly refine these schedules in SyclKittens to build a kernel suite that reaches ~96% of oneDNN in geometric mean across GEMM shapes. The suite runs Llama-3.1-8B inference 1.59x faster than torch$.$compile on one GPU and up to 2.91x faster than a matched multi-GPU decode path on Intel's oneCCL. SyclKittens is open source and available at https://github.com/intel/SyclKittens.

</details>


### [89] [Budget-Constrained Fault-Tolerant Mutual Visibility for Autonomous Robots under the Mobility Fault Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04402)
*Prakhar Shukla,Animesh Maiti,Shivam Kumar,Subhash Bhagat*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文研究了受限于有限能量预算且可能出现任意数量永久性移动故障的机器人 Swarm 中具有遮挡特性的互视问题，并提出了一个 deterministic 的算法，通过 12 种发光颜色实现了非故障机器人的互视和碰撞自由，同时保证了每个机器人的运动预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当机器人缺乏共同坐标系统且存在能量和移动故障时，确保每个非故障机器人都能“看到”系统中所有其他机器人（包括故障机器人）是一个具有挑战性的分布式问题。现有工作通常忽略预算或故障约束，无法处理遮挡和非刚性运动。因此，设计一个无需任何协议且能高效（12 色）解决上述问题的方法具有重要意义。

Method: 算法利用共同固定参考点和 SSYNC 异步光同步模型，分配移动预算并协调每个机器人以达到不被遮挡的互视位置。尽管可能出现任意数量的永久性移动故障，但算法动态地规划路径，确保碰撞自由，并严格限制每个机器人所走的总路程以保持其预算限制。算法采用 deterministic 规则和有限颜色集（12 种颜色），确保在所有非故障机器人在有限时间内协调就位。

Result: 该算法已验证可以在存在任意数量移动故障时成功解决互视问题；所有非故障机器人最终都能彼此可见（包括故障机器人）；每个机器人的移动总距离均不超过其预定的预算；移动路径避免碰撞；且通信仅使用 12 种颜色。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了一种鲁棒且预算敏感的分布式解决方案，适用于具有遮挡特性的机器人 Swarm，能够处理 SSYNC 环境中的非刚性运动和任意数量的移动故障。提出的方法为实现具有可视约束的任务（如协调和监测）的小型自主系统的可靠部署奠定了基础。

Abstract: We investigate the mutual visibility problem for a swarm of $n\ge3$ autonomous mobile robots under the budget-constrained mobility fault model. The robots are opaque, so if three robots are collinear, the middle robot obstructs the visibility between the other two. Each robot is assigned a finite movement budget, reflecting its limited energy, that bounds the total distance it may traverse during the execution. Moreover, an arbitrary number of robots may become permanently immobile due to mobility faults. The objective is to design a distributed algorithm that enables the non-faulty robots to coordinate their movements so that, within a finite time, every non-faulty robot attains unobstructed visibility of all robots in the system, including the faulty ones, while respecting the prescribed movement budget. We consider luminous robots operating under the $\mathsf{SSYNC}$ model with non-rigid movements, without any agreement on their local coordinate systems, and equipped only with a {\it common fixed reference point}. We present a deterministic distributed algorithm that solves the problem despite an arbitrary number of mobility faults. The algorithm guarantees mutual visibility for the non-faulty robots, respects the movement budget of every robot, provides collision-free movements for the robots, and uses only 12 light colors.

</details>


### [90] [AID: A Framework for AI Infrastructure Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04801)
*Abi Aryan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出AID（AI Infrastructure Dynamics）框架，用于描述AI推理基础设施的学习问题，涵盖物理、计算、网络和服务过程。框架支持结构化可变状态、异步观察、多时间尺度和响应服务的需求，并区分预测、服务结果保持和干预响应三种表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏统一框架描述AI推理基础设施的状态、观察信息及支持决策需求， hindering系统性分析与优化。

Method: 建立AID框架，形式化耦合物理-计算-网络-服务过程建模，应用信息论与状态抽象原理推导预测误差下界及可控状态约简充分条件，设计验证协议评估缓存表示、工作负载历史、测量可用性与施加动作。

Result: 获得不可识别模型场景下预测误差下界及精确可控状态约简的充分条件，构建可验证的缓存表示、工作负载历史、测量可用性与施加动作协议。

Conclusion: AID框架为AI推理基础设施提供了系统化的描述与验证方法，有助于支持更可靠的预测、决策与干预。

Abstract: A useful model of AI inference infrastructure must specify the system state, the information available to an observer, and the decisions the model is intended to support. We introduce AID (AI Infrastructure Dynamics), a framework for describing this learning problem across coupled physical, computational, networking, and serving processes. The formulation allows structured and variable-size state, asynchronous observations, multiple physical timescales, and demand that responds to service. We distinguish representations that support prediction under an existing policy from those that preserve service outcomes under changed actions, and separate both from identifying intervention responses. Two analytical results describe a lower bound on prediction error when available observations cannot distinguish models and a sufficient condition for exact controlled state reduction. These results apply established information and state-abstraction principles to AI infrastructure. We then describe a validation protocol for cache representations, workload histories, measurement availability, and imposed actions.

</details>


### [91] [Min-Max Uniform Circle Formation by Asynchronous Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05008)
*Animesh Maiti,Prakhar Shukla,Subhash Bhagat*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究在ASYNC模型下，使点机器人移动到目标圆上形成正n边形且最大单机器人移动距离最小的Min‑Max Uniform Circle Formation (MMUCF)问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作未考虑圆形/均匀圆形形成过程中最大个体位移的优化，而该指标对边界监控、监视、模式形成等实际应用至关重要。

Method: 首先给出确定性解的必要条件，然后在Look‑Compute‑Move框架下（机器人自治、匿名、同质、遗忘、静默且运动非刚性）提出分布式、确定性且无碰撞的算法，先形成任意圆，再在圆上实现均匀分布，同时最小化最大移动距离。

Result: 所提出算法在有限时间内使机器人到达目标圆上不同位置，在均匀情况下达到等距分布的正n边形，并且在所有机器人中最大移动距离达到理论下界（即最小化）。

Conclusion: 该工作为ASYNC模型下的最小最大均匀圆形形成提供了首个确定性分布式解决方案，证明了在受限机器人模型下同时实现形成目标与最小化最大位移的可行性。

Abstract: Given a set of point robots $\mathcal{R}$ in the Euclidean plane and a target circle $\mathbf C$ enclosing all robot positions, the \textsc{Min-Max Uniform Circle Formation (MMUCF)} problem requires the robots to move to distinct positions on $\mathbf C$ such that the final configuration forms a regular $n$-gon while minimizing the maximum distance traveled by any robot. Uniform circle formation is a fundamental coordination task in swarm robotics with applications in perimeter monitoring, surveillance, boundary coverage, and pattern formation. The literature does not address the optimization of the maximum individual displacement during the formation process.
  In this work, we study the min--max versions of the circle formation and uniform circle formation problems, where the goal is to minimize the maximum distance traveled by any robot. We consider these problems under the $\mathcal{ASYNC}$ model, where robots are autonomous, anonymous, identical, homogeneous, oblivious, and silent, and operate under the \textit{Look--Compute--Move} model with non-rigid motion. We first give necessary conditions for a deterministic solution and then present deterministic, distributed, and collision-free algorithms that form a circle and a uniform circle in finite time while minimizing the maximum movement. The algorithms ensure that robots reach distinct positions on the circle and, in the uniform case, equally spaced positions on $\mathbf C$ under the considered model.

</details>


### [92] [You Only Convert Twice: Ozaki Scheme II for Chained Tensor Mode Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05059)
*Xuanzhengbo Ren*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出OzII-RescaleBE方法,在Ozaki scheme II中实现张量模式乘积链的持久执行,通过在残余表示上直接进行重新缩放和基扩展,避免重复中间转换,使用CuTe DSL和INT8 Tensor Core实现,在多个数据集上与GEMMul8-KRON和GEMMul8-Composed相比取得更高吞吖量和较低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在张量模式乘积链执行中重复进行残余到浮点数的转换,导致性能下降。OzII-RescaleBE旨在通过仅在链开始时转换为残余表示、在结束时重构,并在中间直接对残余表示进行重新缩放和基扩展,从而减少转换开销并保持数值精度。

Method: 该方法基于Ozaki scheme II保持中间张量的残余形式,并在残余域内执行重新缩放(rescaling)和基扩展(base extension),从而避免中间向浮点数的转换。使用CuTe DSL和INT8 Tensor Core进行实现,并与GEMMul8-Composed(每模式组合)和GEMMul8-KRON(稠密Kronecker积)进行比较,在链深度d=3~7下误差为2.5×10^-16至2.6×10^-15,吞吖量达1.47~4.42 GDoF/s。

Result: OzII-RescaleBE在链深度d=3至7时实现范数相对误差从2.5×10^-16至2.6×10^-15,优于GEMMul8-Composed;在d=8时误差升至7×10^-14至6×10^-13。吞吖量在n=8至256时达到1.47~4.42 GDoF/s,与GEMMul8-KRON相当(较8%内),并在n≤128时比GEMMul8-Composed快2.1~60倍,但在n=256时低6~12%。内存使用也优于GEMMul8基线。

Conclusion: OzII-RescaleBE通过残余域内的重新缩放和基扩展,实现了高精度且高性能的张量模式乘积链,显著降低了中间转换开销,在大多数情况下超越或媲美现有GEMMul8方法,证明了残余-重新缩放-基扩展范式在持久张量链执行中的有效性。

Abstract: This work proposes OzII-RescaleBE, a method that enables persistent execution of chained tensor mode products in Ozaki scheme II, keeping the intermediate tensors in residue form. By performing rescaling and base extension directly on the residue representation, it requires conversion to residues only at the beginning of the chain and reconstruction only at the end. An implementation using CuTe DSL and INT8 Tensor Cores is provided for verification and evaluation. The method is compared with per-mode composition of Ozaki scheme~II (GEMMul8-Composed) and a dense Kronecker-product formulation (GEMMul8-KRON), both implemented using the GEMMul8 library. Across three datasets with different input distributions, OzII-RescaleBE achieves normwise relative errors ranging from $2.5\times10^{-16}$ to $2.6\times10^{-15}$ for chain depths $d=3$ through $7$. The corresponding errors range from $10^{-16}$ to $10^{-15}$ for GEMMul8-KRON and from $1\times10^{-16}$ to $3\times10^{-16}$ for GEMMul8-Composed and the FP64 chain. At $d=8$, the errors of OzII-RescaleBE increase to between $7\times10^{-14}$ and $6\times10^{-13}$. For mode sizes $n=8$ to $256$, OzII-RescaleBE achieves throughputs ranging from 1.47 to 4.42\,GDoF/s. Its throughput is within 12\,\% of GEMMul8-KRON at $n=8$ and $12$ and exceeds it at larger tested sizes. Compared with GEMMul8-Composed, OzII-RescaleBE achieves $2.1$--$60\times$ the throughput for $n\leq128$, but has 6--12\,\% lower throughput at $n=256$. It also uses less device memory than the GEMMul8 baselines in the measured memory comparisons. These results demonstrate that rescaling and base extension enable accurate residue-domain tensor chains while avoiding repeated intermediate conversions to floating point.

</details>


### [93] [CommuteProp: Decoupled Training for Communication Bound Split LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05105)
*CHEN Ding,LUO Haochen,LIU Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出CommuteProp，一种异步分割学习算法，通过重叠计算与通信来缓解顺序瓶颈，提高吞吐量同时保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统同步分割学习中客户端因等待服务器梯度而空闲，导致训练效率低下；需要一种能够并行执行前向/反向传播和本地权重更新的方法。

Method: CommuteProp将训练分为两个并发阶段：跨块前向-后向传播（与服务器通信）和块内权重更新（本地计算），利用异步流水线重叠计算与通信，并引入基于分析的NS preconditioner以降低丢失导致的噪声。

Result: 实验表明，算法在多种LLM微调任务上实现了显著的吞吐量提升（例如提升2-3倍），而精度与同步基线相当；且可作为即插即用模块与其他隐私增强技术协同工作。

Conclusion: CommuteProp通过有效解除通信-计算顺序依赖，为隐私保护的LLM fine‑tuning提供了一种高效且兼容的异步分割学习范式。

Abstract: Split learning has emerged as a promising paradigm for privacy-preserving LLM fine-tuning, yet its practical deployment is severely hindered by the sequential communication-computation bottleneck. In conventional synchronous pipelines, clients remain idle while waiting for server-side gradients, resulting in substantial training inefficiency. We propose CommuteProp, an asynchronous split-learning algorithm that decouples the training process into two concurrent phases: a cross-block forward-backward pass and an in-block weight update. By overlapping computation with communication, CommuteProp reduces the marginal cycle time from a sum of all stage latencies to the dominant computational bottleneck. We provide a rigorous asynchronous error and convergence analysis. Moreover, we derive an NS preconditioner method based on our analysis to further mitigate staleness-induced noise. Comprehensive experiments indicate that our algorithm achieves substantial throughput gains while maintaining accuracy comparable to synchronous methods; furthermore, it functions as a plug-and-play module that not only accommodates but actively enhances existing privacy enhancement methods.

</details>


### [94] [Characterizing Parallelism Strategies in LLM Inference: Fundamental Compute-Communication Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05305)
*Javad Mirzaei,Jeebak Mitra*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一个统一的分析框架，用于建模分布式LLM推理下的张量并行（TP）、流水线并行（PP）和混合并行（HB）。该框架将端到端延迟分解为计算、GPU间通信和流水线气泡开销，并通过分析模型、工作负载和硬件特性来捕捉不同并行策略的执行行为。实验验证了该模型在多GPU平台上的有效性，确认了并行策略之间的基本计算-通信权衡，并为未来LLM服务系统的选择和优化提供了实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理已成为现代AI系统的核心工作负载，需要服务基础设施在最大化吞吐量的同时满足严格的延迟服务级别目标（SLO）。由于当前SOTA LLM超出了单个GPU的计算和内存容量，推理通常需要跨多个GPU使用张量并行（TP）、流水线并行（PP）或混合并行（HB）。然而，选择最有效的并行策略仍然具有挑战性，因为计算、通信、流水线利用率、序列长度、批处理大小和模型架构之间存在复杂相互作用。现有方法主要依赖经验评估，缺乏对这些策略之间权衡的深入分析，特别是在预填充和解码阶段的差异表现上。

Method: 本文提出一个统一的分析框架，该框架将端到端延迟分解为计算、GPU间通信和流水线气泡开销。它推导了针对TP集体通信、PP点对点通信和流水线利用率的解析模型，这些模型作为硬件、模型和工作负载特征的函数。此外，该框架还区分了预填充和解码阶段的不同执行行为，解释了为什么PP导向配置偏好计算密集型的预填充，而TP导向配置则通过消除流水线气泡来降低解码延迟。

Result: 通过在现代LLM上进行实验验证，该模型在多GPU平台上得到了确认。研究确认了不同并行策略之间的基本计算-通信权衡，并为LLM服务系统的并行策略选择、容量规划和优化提供了实用指导。框架不仅提供了理论洞察，还帮助理解不同并行策略在实际部署中的性能特征。

Conclusion: 该研究成功构建了一个全面的分析框架，为理解和优化分布式LLM推理提供了理论基础。通过提供解析模型和实证验证，该工作为LLM服务系统的设计和优化提供了重要参考，帮助研究人员和工程师更科学地选择并行策略以实现最佳性能平衡。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) inference has become the dominant workload in modern AI systems, requiring serving infrastructures to maximize throughput while meeting strict latency Service-Level Objectives (SLOs). Since state-of-the-art LLMs exceed the compute and memory capacity of a single GPU, inference is commonly distributed across multiple GPUs using tensor parallelism (TP), pipeline parallelism (PP), or hybrid parallelism (HB). However, selecting the most effective parallelism strategy remains challenging due to complex interactions among computation, communication, pipeline utilization, sequence length, batch size, and model architecture. Existing approaches largely rely on empirical evaluation and provide limited analytical insight into the trade-offs among these strategies, particularly across the distinct prefill and decoding phases of inference. In this paper, we present a unified analytical framework for modeling distributed LLM inference under TP, PP, and HB. The framework decomposes end-to-end latency into computation, inter-GPU communication, and pipeline bubble overhead, and derives analytical models that capture TP collective communication, PP point-to-point communication, and pipeline utilization as functions of hardware, model, and workload characteristics. The model further characterizes the differing execution behavior of prefill and decoding, explaining why PP-oriented configurations favor compute-intensive prefill while TP-oriented configurations reduce decoding latency by eliminating pipeline bubbles. Experiments with modern LLMs on multi-GPU platforms validate the model and confirm the fundamental compute-communication trade-off across parallelism strategies. The framework provides practical guidance for parallelism selection, capacity planning, and optimization of future LLM serving systems.

</details>


### [95] [HiNa-MoE: High-Performance, Non-Intrusive MoE Inference on CPUs with Matrix Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05123)
*Weiling Yang,Junwen Zhang,Dezun Dong,Jianbin Fang,Enda Yu,Zhe Bai,Xiaopeng Deng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了 HiNa-MoE，一个基于 Intel AMX 的非入侵式高性能 MoE 推理 CPU 运算库，通过优化微内核、轻量布局转换融合、NUMA 感知任务分区以及将解码阶段的矩阵向量运算转化为小矩阵矩阵运算，在无需修改框架分配器的情况下实现了可移植性与性能的平衡，并在多个 MoE 模型上实现了最高 3.37 倍 FFN 内核加速与 2.09 倍端到端推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在本地和内部部署环境中，MoE 推理的专家参数常超出 GPU 显存容量。在延迟敏感、低并发场景下，反复将专家权重从 CPU 内存调度到 GPU 成本高昂，使得路由 FFN 成为多插槽 CPU 的关键路径。现有的 CPU 加速方案多依赖硬件或拓扑特定要求（如 AMX 权重布局或手动 NUMA 感知放置），降低了可移植性并增加了与标准 CPU-GPU 卸载管线的集成复杂性。

Method: HiNa-MoE 通过三种核心技术实现高性能与非入侵性：(1) 利用优化的 AMX 微内核，在标准权重布局上融入轻量布局转换到 token gathering 与存储环节，无需重排权重；(2) 在不修改框架分配器的前提下，采用简单的页交错策略实现 NUMA 感知任务分区；(3) 将解码阶段的内存矩阵-向量运算转化为小规模矩阵-矩阵执行，以充分利用 AMX 算力。

Result: 在多个 MoE 模型上评估显示，HiNa-MoE 相比 state-of-the-art 基线，FFN 内核最高加速 3.37 倍，端到端推理最高加速 2.09 倍。该库兼容现有框架与部署工作流，即插即用，无需修改底层编排系统。

Conclusion: HiNa-MoE 展示了在标准 CPU 硬件上实现高效、可移植 MoE 推理的可行性，为受限环境下的大模型部署提供了低门槛、高性能的解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) inference is increasingly deployed in local and on-premise environments, where expert parameters often exceed GPU memory capacity. In latency-sensitive, low-concurrency settings, repeatedly staging routed-expert weights from CPU memory to the GPU can be prohibitive, leaving routed-expert feed-forward networks (FFNs) on the critical path of multi-socket CPUs. Existing CPU accelerations often rely on intrusive, hardware- or topology-specific requirements, such as AMX-specific weight layouts or manual NUMA-aware placement. These requirements reduce portability and complicate integration with standard CPU-GPU offloading pipelines. We present HiNa-MoE, a high-performance, non-intrusive operator library for MoE inference on CPUs with Intel AMX. HiNa-MoE (1) exploits AMX with an optimized micro-kernel that keeps expert weights in standard layouts and instead fuses lightweight layout transforms into token gathering and stores; (2) applies NUMA-aware task partitioning under a simple page-interleaved policy without modifying the framework allocator; and (3) converts decode-phase memory matrix-vector operations into small matrix-matrix execution to utilize AMX. Across multiple MoE models, HiNa-MoE achieves up to 3.37x speedup for FFN kernels and up to 2.09x end-to-end inference speedup over state-of-the-art baselines, while remaining plug-and-play with existing frameworks and deployment workflows.

</details>


### [96] [Acceleration of Data Analytics on Heterogeneous Supercloud Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06291)
*Georgios Zacharopoulos,Ilias Bournias,Lukas Cavigelli*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种名为 ReDSEa 的工具链，针对异构 Supercloud 系统，自动优化密集 Cholesky 分解和 SVD 分析的性能，实现了 CPU 实现上高达 17 倍和 88 倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构 Supercloud 系统在数据分析中的应用日益增长，但现有的计算模型和性能估计技术难以有效支持密集 Cholesky 分解和 SVD 分析。因此，开发自动化的映射、负载均衡、调度、并行化和计算重叠技术对于充分发挥异构资源的优势至关重要。

Method: 本文提出了专为加速 Cholesky 分解和 SVD 分析设计的计算模型和性能估计技术，并开发了一个名为 ReDSEa 的自动化工具链。该工具链通过 LLVM 编译器实现了递归、迭代和分块计算的新型性能模型，能够在华为 Kunpeng 920 ARM CPU 和 Ascend 910 AI 加速器等异构系统中自动处理映射、负载均衡、调度、并行化和计算重叠等任务。

Result: 实验证明，在使用 48 个核心的高度优化 CPU 实现进行基准测试时，所提出的方法实现了 Cholesky 分解高达 17 倍的速度提升，SVD 则实现了高达 88 倍的速度提升。

Conclusion: ReDSEa 工具链为异构 Supercloud 系统上的密集线性代数操作提供了一种高效且自动化的解决方案。通过综合利用基于 LLVM 的编译技术和先进的性能建模技术，该方法能够显著加速 Cholesky 分解和 SVD 分析，从而推动了大型规模数据分析的发展。

Abstract: Heterogeneous Supercloud systems are transforming data analytics by enabling scalable and efficient task distribution across diverse resources. This paper presents computational models and performance estimation techniques tailored for accelerating Dense Cholesky (CH) Decomposition and Singular Value Decomposition (SVD)-based analytics on Heterogeneous Superclouds. Our ReDSEa tool-chain automates mapping, load balancing, scheduling, parallelism, and computation overlap. Implemented on a heterogeneous system with a Huawei Kunpeng 920 ARM CPU and an Ascend 910 AI accelerator, our LLVM compiler tool-chain employs novel performance models for recursive, iterative, and blocked computations, achieving up to 17x speedup for CH and 88x for SVD over fully optimized 48-core CPU implementations.

</details>


### [97] [Fine-Tuning a 3B-Parameter LLM on a Smartphone: Characterizing Sustained Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06325)
*Andrew Geyko,Marius Mosbach,André Brinkmann*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 首次系统性地对在移动设备上微调的多亿参数大模型进行了表征，显示 iPhone 17 Pro 可在一次电池充电内完成 3B 参数 LLM 的微调，产生的 Adapter 个人化效果可与服务器训练的相当。长时间训练使手机性能持续降至初始 throughput 的一半，测试的暂停/爆发调度均无法恢复。绝大多数训练步骗时间耗在冻结的基模型反向传播上，这九个被审计的其他运行时不加速。Apple 的 MLX 有从未被调用且错误的内核，我们的修复已合并上游，可在使用三分之一更少能量的情况下 1.47 倍更快地训练 Adapter。如今场景可行，但需运行时和操作系统将训练视为一等工作负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的工作仅在手机上测量过单个训练步骤，未覆盖完整训练过程，也未验证设备上训练的 Adapter 是否能提升个人化效果。目标是实现在不将用户数据离开手机的情况下对大模型进行个性化微调。

Method: 系统表征在 iPhone 17 Pro 上微调 3B 参数 LLM 的内存、每步耗时、热行为和能耗；测量长时间训练后的吞吐量变化；测试暂停与爆发调度策略；审计十余运行时的加速情况；定位并修复 Apple MLX 中的内核错误。

Result: iPhone 17 Pro 可在一次电池充电内完成 3B LLM 微调；Adapter 的个人化效果与服务器训练的无异；长时间训练使手机吞吐量持续降至约一半，暂停/爆发策略无法扭转；每步绝大多数时间花在冻结基模型的反向传播上，九其它运行时不加速该部分；MLX 修复使 Adapter 训练速度 1.47 倍，能耗降低三分之一。

Conclusion: 当前手机已可行对大模型进行端侧微调，但要提高效率，运行时和操作系统需要将训练视为一等工作负载。

Abstract: Multi-billion-parameter LLMs now run on phones for inference, and training them on the device would personalize them without user data leaving the phone. Prior work has measured individual training steps of such models on phones, but not complete training runs, and not whether adapters trained on the device improve personalization. We present the first systematic characterization of a multi-billion-parameter LLM fine-tuned on a mobile device, covering memory, per-step time, thermal behavior, and energy. An iPhone 17 Pro can fine-tune a 3B-parameter LLM to a typical user within one battery charge, and the resulting adapters improve personalization as much as adapters trained on a server. Sustained training throttles the phone to about half its initial throughput, and none of the pausing or burst schedules we tested recovers it. Nearly all of each training step is spent in the frozen base model, most of it in the backward pass, which nine of the ten other runtimes we audited do not accelerate. Apple's MLX had a kernel for it that was never dispatched and was incorrect, and our repair, now merged upstream, trains an adapter 1.47x faster on a third less energy. On-device fine-tuning is feasible on current phones, and making it efficient requires runtimes and operating systems to treat training as a first-class workload.

</details>


### [98] [Optimistic Reliable Broadcast in Three Steps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05445)
*Nikita Polyanskii*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 研究无签名异步可靠广播中乐观两步交付的延迟权衡，提出两种构造并证明匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在异步拜占庭容错环境下，如何在保持最大乐观故障预算的同时优化延迟。

Method: 定义(Do,Ds,Dt)三个延迟指标，针对3f+1≤n<4f设计协议并证明确定性下界。

Result: 将Opt-RBC的(2,4,4)改进为(2,3,4)；达到(2,3,3)匹配下界；证明总延迟至少为3。

Conclusion: 在匹配范围内，最大乐观性仅增加一个总延迟步骤，不增加良好情况延迟，协议使用二次消息并容忍自适应腐败。

Abstract: We study the latency tradeoffs of signature-free asynchronous reliable broadcast with optimistic two-step delivery. We consider $n$ parties, of which at most $f$ are Byzantine. An optimistic fault budget $b\le f$ guarantees two-step delivery with an honest broadcaster and at most $b$ faults, while correctness tolerates $f$ faults. We write $(d_o,d_s,d_t)$ for optimistic latency, honest-broadcaster latency with up to $f$ faults, and totality delay-the elapsed time from first to last honest delivery.
  For $3f+1\le n<4f$, our first construction improves Opt-RBC's $(2,4,4)$ guarantees to $(2,3,4)$, preserving its maximal optimistic budget $b_*=\lfloor n/2\rfloor-f$. Thus maximal optimism is compatible with the optimal three-step good case.
  We prove a complementary lower bound: for $3f+1\le n<4f$, every deterministic reliable broadcast protocol in our asynchronous model with two-step optimistic budget $b\ge n-3f$ has totality delay at least three, even when the broadcaster is honest. Our second construction achieves $(2,3,3)$, matching this lower bound at maximal optimism when $f\ge2$ and $\lceil(10f-3)/3\rceil\le n<4f$. Both constructions use quadratic messages and tolerate adaptive corruption. In the matching range, maximal optimism therefore costs exactly one totality step relative to Bracha's $(3,3,2)$, without increasing good-case latency.

</details>


### [99] [Rethinking Streaming-Perception Evaluation on Heterogeneous Edge Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05578)
*Misun Yu,Jinyoung Moon,Jemin Lee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文针对异构边缘平台（GPU-NPU）上的多摄像头流式感知部署，指出传统孤立单流评估和平均sAP会误导加速器放置决策，通过实验揭示了GPU竞争导致的截止期丢失会逆转最优放置（从All-GPU转向All-NPU），并建议结合最差流sAP和截止期丢失率进行综合评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多摄像头流式感知系统日益部署在与共驻工作负载共享的异构边缘平台上，但加速器放置策略通常仅基于孤立的单流实验和平均流式平均精度（mean sAP）进行评估，这无法反映实际多流并发和资源竞争下的截止期丢失与单流退化。

Method: 在单GPU-NPU平台上构建两套端到端管道，通过多流并发实验和引入GPU饱和的视觉-语言共驻负载，系统对比了All-GPU和All-NPU放置策略在不同竞争强度下的流式平均精度（sAP）、延迟及截止日期丢失情况。

Result: 研究表明，孤立环境下GPU管道更优，但GPU本地竞争会导致严重的截止期丢失，逆转放置偏好。随着GPU侧竞争加剧，最佳放置从All-GPU转向All-NPU；在GPU饱和共驻负载下，All-NPU的最差流sAP是All-GPU的5.2倍。大尺寸物体在竞争和延迟实验中遭受了最大的绝对sAP损失。

Conclusion: 平均sAP会掩盖严重的单流性能退化。为准确评估系统在真实竞争环境下的表现，评估应同时报告竞争扫描、双路径的截止日期错过率以及最差流sAP，而非仅依赖平均指标。

Abstract: Multi-camera streaming perception is increasingly deployed on heterogeneous edge platforms shared with co-resident workloads, yet accelerator placement is often evaluated using isolated single-stream experiments and mean streaming average precision (sAP). Using two end-to-end pipelines on a single GPU--NPU platform, we show that isolated evaluation can mis-rank deployment-time placement. Although the GPU pipeline is preferred in isolation, GPU-localized contention introduces deadline misses that make detections stale and can reverse the preferred placement before full GPU saturation. The NPU pipeline is less accurate than the GPU pipeline on small and medium objects in isolation, but nearly matches it on large objects. The largest absolute sAP losses in our latency and contention experiments occur for large objects. In our four-stream experiments, the preferred placement depends on which path becomes stale, and increasing GPU-side contention shifts the best placement from All-GPU to All-NPU. Under a GPU-saturating vision--language co-tenant, All-NPU achieves $5.2\times$ the worst-stream sAP of All-GPU. Because mean sAP can hide severe single-stream degradation, evaluation should report contention sweeps, deadline-miss rates on both paths, and worst-stream sAP alongside mean sAP.

</details>


### [100] [Handling Missing Data in Performance Portability Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05605)
*Ami Marowka*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究评估了多种插补方法以解决高性能计算（HPC）性能可移植性数据集中的缺失数据问题，并提供了实用建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 性能可移植性评分依赖完整的性能效率数据集，但实际中常因基准测试不完整、硬件约束或实现差距导致数据缺失。现有指标缺乏处理缺失输入的机制，导致分析不完整或无法计算评分。

Method: 评估了一系列插补方法和算法，并通过真实的HPC性能可移植性数据集进行实例展示和应用分析。

Result: 结果揭示了不同插补方法在准确性、鲁棒性和计算成本之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 为研究人员提供了减轻缺失数据对性能可移植性评估影响的实用建议，帮助其在准确性和计算成本间做出权衡。

Abstract: Missing data is a common challenge in many real-world datasets, often leading to biased results or reduced accuracy. Missing data is a pervasive problem in performance efficiency datasets, particularly in high-performance computing (HPC) performance portability studies. Performance portability scores rely on complete sets of performance efficiencies as inputs to their respective metrics; however, missing values can arise due to incomplete benchmarking, hardware constraints, or implementation gaps. Many established performance portability metrics lack mechanisms for handling missing inputs, which can result in incomplete analyses or the inability to compute scores altogether.
  In this study, we evaluate a range of imputation methods and algorithms for addressing missing performance efficiencies. We present illustrative examples for each approach and apply them to real-world HPC performance portability datasets. Our results highlight the trade-offs between accuracy, robustness, and computational cost, offering practical recommendations for researchers seeking to mitigate the impact of missing data in performance portability assessments.

</details>


### [101] [Nexus: An Execution Fabric for AI Agents Across Cloud, Edge, and Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05709)
*Cary Chang,Jialin Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Nexus 是一个云边协同平台，将每个调用视为持久化任务，利用 OpenWrt-based 运行时、运行范围委托和持久化记录，解决中心化执行的失败风险、扩压力和成本问题，实现跨设备统一执行与生命周期管理。实验显示跨设备工作流成功率高，吊销测试有效，日志记录消除重复写入，Nexus 在匹配任务对中完成率和速度均优于对标系统，且在更低内存上运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Agent 框架简化了推理和工具调用，但云-centric 设计面临三大局限：中心化执行增加失败影响、扩压力和计算成本；跨电脑/移动/边缘设备扩展需要统一的执行抽象和权限控制；长时间运行需要跨故障恢复、结果、计费的生命周期管理。

Method: Nexus 平台使用 OpenWrt-based runtime 进行分布式 serving，run-scoped delegation 授权访问计算和移动环境，持久化记录追踪执行、输出、失败、恢复、使用和结算，协调云和边缘组件间的任务执行。

Result: 实验评估显示：所有 10 个 Computer-Android 工作流成功；所有 6 个吊销测试阻止后续写入同时保留 prior authorized reads。在 worker loss 下，journaling 将重复追加从 6 次降至 0，每任务增加 0.933 s 均值正常路径开销。在 24 个匹配任务对中，Nexus 完成 24 任务对比 Dify 的 22，联合成功对上 median 快 3.88 s。在另一工作负载中，Nexus 的测试增量运行内存上限仅 16 MiB，低于 Dapr 的 64 MiB，虽然 Dapr 实现了更低的成功调用延迟。

Conclusion: 结果表明，局部性、操作范围权威和持久结果识别支持云边 Agent 服务，但成本取决于工作负载特性。

Abstract: Language-model agents are evolving into long-running services that interact with models, tools, computers, mobile devices, and distributed environments. Existing agent frameworks simplify reasoning and tool invocation. However, cloud-centric designs face three limitations: centralized execution increases failure impact, scaling pressure, and compute cost; extending agents across computers, mobile devices, and edge environments requires a unified execution abstraction with permission control; and long-running executions require consistent lifecycle management across failures, recovery, results, usage, and settlement. We present Nexus, a cloud-edge platform that treats each invocation as a persistent task. Nexus uses an OpenWrt-based runtime for distributed serving, run-scoped delegation for authorized access to Computer and Mobile environments, and persistent records to track execution, outputs, failures, recovery, usage, and charging across cloud and edge components. We evaluate Nexus on controlled, cross-device, and model-driven workloads. All ten Computer-Android workflows succeed, and all six revocation tests block subsequent writes while preserving prior authorized reads. Under worker loss, journaling eliminates duplicate appends (six to zero per task), adding 0.933 s mean normal-path overhead. Across 24 matched task pairs, Nexus completes 24 tasks versus Dify's 22 and is a median 3.88 s faster on jointly successful pairs. In a separate workload, Nexus operates under a smaller tested incremental-runtime memory ceiling than Dapr (16 versus 64 MiB), although Dapr achieves lower successful-call latency. These results demonstrate how locality, operation-scoped authority, and persistent result identity support cloud-edge agent services with workload-dependent costs.

</details>


### [102] [MOLT: A Fine-Grained GPU Memory Sharing System for LLM Serving with Opportunistic Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05748)
*Jaehoon Yang,Yongbeom Kim,Hojoon Kim,Seung Yul Lee,Jae W. Lee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出 MOLT 系统，通过细粒度内存共享实现 LLM 微调与推理的共存，解决 GPU 内存闲置问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 推理副本中 GPU 内存常处于闲置，而自动扩缩容耗时过长无法及时释放；现有协同放置方案回收粒度过粗，需丢弃正在进行的微调步骤。

Method: MOLT 允许推理侧回收微调步骤为反向传播保存的单个激活内存，微调步骤继续运行并重新计算这些激活，同时确保不干涉任何正在进行的 GPU 计算。

Result: 在 24B-70B 模型及 H100/B200 GPU 上的测试显示，推理 SLO 达成率不低于 99.7%，微调工作量完成度达到丢弃式方案的 1.9-3.3 倍。

Conclusion: MOLT 在保证推理服务质量的同时，显著提升了 GPU 内存利用率和微调吞吐能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) serving scales its replica count with the request load, yet GPU memory still stands idle inside the replicas. Adding a replica takes minutes, while the memory that a replica needs changes within seconds. Even instant autoscaling could not return this idle memory, because the smallest unit that it can remove is a whole replica. Colocating parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) with inference can use this memory, but inference must be able to reclaim it within seconds, before requests that wait for memory exceed their latency service-level objective (SLO). Existing colocation systems either keep the tuning memory resident or let inference reclaim it at the coarse granularity of a whole training sample. Each such reclamation also discards the running tuning step. To address these limitations, we present MOLT, a fine-grained memory sharing system that lets inference reclaim the memory of individual activations that a running tuning step has saved for its backward pass. The step continues, and its backward pass recomputes those activations. Inference reclaims only memory that no in-flight GPU work can still access, even under CPU--GPU asynchrony and tensor parallelism. On four model deployments (24B--70B) across H100 SXM and B200 GPUs under trace-driven workloads, MOLT keeps inference SLO attainment at or above 99.7% and completes 1.9--3.3x the tuning work of discard-based memory sharing.

</details>


### [103] [Serve Now or Improve Later? Scheduling Self-Evolution in Online Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06212)
*Yangbo Wei,Junhong Qian,Zhen Huang,Zhenyu Su,Qifan Wang,Shaoqiang Lu,Rumin Zhang,Chen Wu,Lei He*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LearnSched 是一种状态感知调度器，将能力复用窗口与计算回报纳入统一投资模型，以优化在线代理的自进化调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线代理在利用空闲计算进行自进化时面临系统约束：收益只有在产物发布并被使用后才实现，而暂停进化会导致服务容量等待内存释放和运行时恢复，因此需要权衡是否继续或推迟进化。

Method: LearnSched 将候选进度、检查点开销和当前恢复路径纳入动作值，使用单步反事实滚动选择进度、检查点或等待，基于全成本窗口策略进行决策。通过独立 A100 组件测量刻画交接成本，并在 1920 个配对有限模型场景中评估动作选择。

Result: 当冷恢复成本高时，等待更长执行窗口可避免交接，提升净价值；在暖恢复场景中，强窗口策略已达到相同价值。保留可恢复状态可缩短容量返回并减少复杂进化调度的需求。

Conclusion: LearnSched 能够在相同信息与容量约束下，通过统一投资模型有效调度自进化过程，提升系统净价值。

Abstract: Online agents can improve future service by constructing reusable tools, guidance, or model states, but this work competes with current requests for the same GPUs. Exploiting idle compute for self-evolution faces a fundamental systems constraint: benefits arrive only after an artifact is published and used, while pausing evolution leaves service capacity waiting for memory release and runtime recovery. An investment worth completing may therefore be worth postponing. We present LearnSched, a state-aware scheduler that brings the reuse window of a capability and the timely return of compute into a common investment model. LearnSched incorporates candidate progress, checkpoint overhead, and the current recovery path into action values. Under the same information and capacity constraints, it uses one-step counterfactual rollout to choose progress, checkpointing, or waiting relative to a fully costed window policy. We characterize handoff costs through independent A100 component measurements and evaluate action selection in 1920 paired finite-model scenarios. When cold recovery is expensive, waiting for longer execution windows can improve net value by avoiding handoffs; in evaluated warm-recovery scenarios, the strong window policy already achieves the same value. Retaining recoverable state can shorten capacity return and reduce the need for complex evolution scheduling.

</details>


### [104] [From a Last-Week Add-On to First-Day Practice: Three Years of Integrating MPI Performance Analysis into HPC Education with EduMPI Suite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06352)
*Anna-Lena Roth,Jonas Posner*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 EduMPI Suite 的三年应用经验，该工具通过自动化执行、测量和实时可视化 MPI 通信来简化 MPI 程序的性能分析。 研究表明，将性能分析从课程的补充部分转变为 MPI 开发的持续组成部分可以改善学习成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 性能分析对于可扩展的 MPI 程序至关重要，但工具和工作流程的复杂性使其通常仅作为并行编程课程的最后部分讲授。 作者希望降低学习门槛，并研究了将性能分析工具整合到 HPC 教育中的最佳实践。

Method: 他们跟踪了 EduMPI Suite 在三年的教学中的应用，使用了未发表的历史调查数据和 2025 年的一份学生问卷。 对比了早期和后来的学生对性能分析的看法和对工具的评估。

Result: 早期课程的学生认为性能分析是不必要、困难或不直观的。 在最终调查中，33 名学生中有 29-33 人建议将 EduMPI Suite 提前引入，他们报告称，该工具在理解程序行为、解释通信、识别瓶颈、获取可视化反馈和导航方面优于传统工具。

Conclusion: 这些发现支持在 MPI 教育中嵌入性能分析，将其作为常规部分，而不是最后补充，以帮助学生过渡到专业的 HPC 环境。

Abstract: Performance analysis is essential for scalable MPI programs, yet tool and workflow complexity often relegates it to the end of Parallel Programming courses. EduMPI Suite lowers this entry barrier by automating cluster execution, measurement, and near-real-time analysis while visualizing MPI communication from the first program. This paper presents a three-year experience report on its integration into HPC education. We trace the transition of performance analysis from a last-week add-on to a recurring element of MPI development, using unpublished historical survey evidence and individual 2025 questionnaire data. Earlier cohorts perceived performance analysis as unnecessary, difficult, or unintuitive. In the final study, all students recommended introducing EduMPI Suite early in the course. Of 33 students, 29-33 rated it higher than conventional tools for understanding program behavior, interpreting communication, identifying bottlenecks, obtaining visual feedback, and navigating the tool. These findings support embedding performance analysis throughout MPI education before transitioning learners to professional environments.

</details>


### [105] [DIALER: A Case for Improving Rare-Class Accuracy in Retraining-Free Edge Video Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06358)
*Dongyoon Ryu,Sungho Jeon,Xinyue Ma,Di Wang,Jonghyun Choi,Minjia Zhang,Myeongjae Jeon*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DIALER系统通过预构建多阶段修正管道，利用VFM推理释放的空闲计算资源来缓解罕见类别的误分类问题，在四个驾驶数据集上将罕见类别准确率提升了高达14.0%，同时不影响实时VFM推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘视频分析中轻量级模型在面对直播流中的分布偏移时会出现准确率下降问题。虽然持续学习（CL）可以解决数据漂移，但它会严重消耗边缘服务器原本为推理预留的有限计算资源。

Method: DIALER系统利用重训练无需的视觉基础模型（VFMs）推理释放的空闲计算周期。在离线阶段预构建多阶段修正管道以处理常见类别与罕见类别的混淆对；运行时将候选纠正项路由到对应管道，并根据GPU空闲度执行尽可能多的阶段。

Result: 在四个真实世界驾驶数据集上测试，DIALER将罕见类别准确率提高了高达14.0%，且不会干扰多流分析中的实时VFM推理性能。

Conclusion: DIALER提供了一种实用的、无需重新训练的替代方案，通过充分利用现有VFM推理的计算冗余，有效缓解了罕见类别误分问题，同时保持了系统的实时性和效率。

Abstract: Edge video analytics with lightweight models is prone to accuracy degradation due to persistent distributional shifts in live video streams. While continuous learning (CL) addresses such data drift, it heavily strains the limited compute resources of edge servers originally provisioned for inference. Our empirical study reveals that emerging vision foundation models (VFMs) offer a practical, retraining-free alternative that delivers high average accuracy with remarkable compute savings. However, VFMs frequently misclassify specific rare classes, which often represent critical objects, as visually similar common classes. We design DIALER, a system that exploits the spare compute cycles freed by retraining-free VFM inference to mitigate rare-class misclassifications. Specifically, DIALER pre-builds multi-stage correction pipelines for dominant rare-to-common confusion pairs offline. At runtime, it routes correction candidates to the corresponding pipelines and executes as many stages as idle GPU headroom permits. Evaluation on four real-world driving datasets shows that DIALER improves rare-class accuracy by up to 14.0% without interfering with real-time VFM inference for multi-stream analytics.

</details>


### [106] [GPU-Initiated Discrete Simulated Bifurcation: Low-Latency Requests and Streaming Dense Couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06622)
*Yaocheng Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 使用 NVIDIA DOCA GPUNetIO 构建离散模拟分裂 (dSB) 架构，通过 GPU 常驻服务和流式求解器解决频繁请求与超大模型的通信瓶颈，实现低延迟并支持超显存密集矩阵求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 GPU 的优化在高并发请求和需要传输超过显存的密集模型时，受限于 CPU 数据路径和频繁的套接字通信，导致延迟升高和吞吐受限。

Method: 对于常驻模型，利用 DOCA GPUNetIO 构建持久服务，每个请求在单个 GPU 线程块中完成求解，采用整数耦合求和、GPU 工作队列和批量传输，使受理‑求解‑回复全流程在设备端完成；对于超显存模型，采用流式求解器在 GPU 上保持动态状态并复用到来的耦合瓦片，通过 64 MiB 包缓冲区流式传输 12.5 TB 逻辑矩阵，实现约 307 Gb/s 的吞吐。

Result: 在并发负载从 400k 提升到 800k 请求/秒时，中间往返延迟仅上升 6%；最高负载下中位数和 99th‑pct 延迟分别为 189 µs 和 218 µs，优于经过调优的持久 CPU 代理（288 µs / 609 µs）。流式求解器能以约 307 Gb/s 处理千万变量的密集二进制矩阵，通过 64 MiB 缓冲区消费 12.5 TB 逻辑矩阵，且在植入实例上的基态恢复与参考实验结果一致。

Conclusion: dSB 架构通过两种模式（常驻服务 + 流式求解器）成功消除了通信瓶颈，实现了高并发低延迟请求处理以及超显存密集模型的求解，为 GPU 加速的大规模优化提供了可扩展方案。

Abstract: GPU-based optimization faces two communication bottlenecks: coordinating frequent requests and delivering dense models that exceed device memory. We present a discrete simulated bifurcation (dSB) architecture that addresses both through NVIDIA DOCA GPUNetIO. For resident models, a persistent service receives field updates, executes each solve within one GPU thread block, and returns the result. Exact integer coupling sums, GPU work queues, and batched transmission keep the receive--solve--reply path on the device without a dedicated CPU data-path core. In comparisons with socket-based servers using the same solver, the largest latency gains occur under concurrent load. As the offered load increases from 400 to 800 thousand requests per second, median round-trip latency rises by only 6\%. At the highest tested load, median and 99th-percentile latencies are 189 and 218~$μ$s, compared with 288 and 609~$μ$s for the tuned persistent CPU proxy across repeated runs. For models larger than device memory, a streaming solver retains dynamical state on the GPU and reuses incoming coupling tiles across replicas. It evaluates ten-million-variable dense binary matrices at approximately 307~Gb/s, consuming a 12.5-TB logical matrix through a 64-MiB packet buffer. Ground-state recovery on planted instances and agreement with reference executions verify the computation. Together, the two modes scale dSB to concurrent requests and dense models beyond GPU memory.

</details>


### [107] [OrigaMIG: MIG-Aware VM Placement with a Neighborhood-Restricted BILP and Live Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06646)
*Ahmad Siavashi,Mahmoud Momtazpour*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文研究了如何更好地在支持多实例 GPU 的云数据中心中放置虚拟机。作者将 MIG-aware VM 放置建模为二元整数线性规划问题，并提出了一种名为 OrigaMIG 的解决方案，该方案使用轻量级贪心算法进行初始放置，只有在必要时（如无法放置请求或 GPU 碎片化时）才调用求解器，并在物理机的小型邻居中求解 BILP 以通过实时迁移执行解决方案。实验结果表明，OrigaMIG 能够保持更少的物理机活跃，迁移的 VM 更少，能效更高，在多种工作负载和硬件配置下表现优异，与最先进的方法相比，平均可将 VM 迁移减少 29‑ 49%，并使用最少的每 GPU‑hour 能量消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 LLM 服务的发展，多租户环境对 GPU 资源的共享需求日益增长，Nvidia 的 MIG 技术提供了隔离的 GPU 实例，但其放置规则往往导致碎片化和低效的资源利用率。论文旨在开发一种能够最大限度地提高请求接受率、整合资源和减少迁移开销的 MIG-aware VM 放置解决方案。

Method: 首先将 MIG-aware VM 放置建模为二元整数线性规划 (BILP) 问题，以最大化请求接受率。其次，提出 OrigaMIG 优化器，该优化器使用轻量级贪心算法一次性放置每个新请求，并在无法放置请求或 GPU 碎片化时仅在物理机的小型邻居中调用 BILP 求解器以执行实时迁移，从而保持数据中心其余部分固定。

Result: 在 256 块 A100/A30 GPU（128 个物理机）上，OrigaMIG 与最先进的方法相比，在 30 次成对运行中，有 29 次保持更少的物理机活跃状态，VM 迁移减少 29‑ 49%，并在所有负载下实现最低的每 GPU‑hour 能耗。与默认放置策略相比，OrigaMIG 可将活跃物理机数量减少多达 12.5%，并实现多达 7.8 个百分点的 GPU 内存请求接受率提升。在小型数据中心中，OrigaMIG 的活跃物理机数量保持在最优值的 4.1% 以内。

Conclusion: 论文提出了一种新的 MIG-aware VM 放置优化方法 OrigaMIG，该方法通过智能资源整合和最小化迁移开销，显著提高了云数据中心在 MIG 环境下的 GPU 资源利用率和能效表现，验证了其在真实场景中的有效性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: The extensive use of GPUs in cloud computing, accelerated by the spread of large language model (LLM) services, and the growing need for multitenancy have driven the development of innovative solutions for efficient GPU resource management. Multi-Instance GPU (MIG) technology from NVIDIA enables shared GPU usage in cloud data centers by providing isolated instances, which are offered as MIG-backed virtual GPUs (vGPUs). However, MIG placement rules often lead to fragmentation and suboptimal resource allocation. In this work, we formally model the MIG-aware virtual machine (VM) placement as a binary integer linear programming (BILP) problem aimed at maximizing request acceptance, consolidating resources, and reducing migration overhead. Building upon this formulation, we propose OrigaMIG, a MIG-aware placement optimizer. OrigaMIG places each arriving request at once with a lightweight greedy rule and calls the solver only when a request cannot be placed or the GPUs of a class become fragmented. Each call solves the BILP on a small neighborhood of physical machines (PMs), keeps the rest of the data center fixed, and executes the solution as live migrations. We compare OrigaMIG with the default placement and with two state-of-the-art MIG-aware policies in simulation, across six loads, eleven variants of the workload and the hardware, and data centers of up to 4096 PMs. On 256 A100 and A30 GPUs in 128 PMs, OrigaMIG keeps fewer PMs active than the stronger policy in 29 of 30 paired runs while migrating 29% to 49% fewer VMs, and it uses the least energy per admitted GPU-hour at every load. Against the default placement, it keeps up to 12.5% fewer PMs active and admits up to 7.8 percentage points more of the requested GPU memory. On small data centers, it stays within 4.1% of the optimal number of active PMs.

</details>


### [108] [One Global Beam Across Many GPUs: High-Throughput Beam Search at Billion-Record Frontier Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06718)
*Ivan Litvak*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种分布式束搜索算法，使多个 GPU 能协同执行搜索，即使保留集超出单设备容量。实现将状态和候选保留在 GPU 上，CPU 仅处理少量控制与祖先记录。理论证明了在特定条件下（全局键值归约、无竞争路由、Hash128 等价、完整物理布局 tie-break）可得到与单机相同的 top-B 结果。实验在 H200、T4、RTX 3060 等硬件上测得高吞吐与加速比，但未解决目标谜题（深度 9），且不同任务/硬件间不构成强扩展性证明。历史实现存在未解的跨缓冲唯一性与多秩分发风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决束搜索在大规模保留集（B 值极大）时单 GPU 内存不足的问题，实现多 GPU 协同搜索，同时保持与单机算法结果完全一致的正确性保证。

Method: 设计分布式流水线：各 GPU 本地生成子节点、去重、保留 top-B；通过全局键值归约（每键一次）和无竞争路由跨设备合并；使用 Hash128 等价类与完全指定的物理布局 tie-break 确保确定性；CPU 仅收集微小的控制与祖先信息。并对历史单 T4 实现进行静态审计，发现潜在缺陷。

Result: 8 张 H200 在 Cube4 任务上以 B_eff≈29 亿完成饱和深度 8，耗时 931.266 秒，吞吐 74.746 百万 parent-generator 对/秒；深度 9 用户中止。2 张 T4 在 Megaminx 上达 30.274 百万逻辑子节点/秒（B_eff≈8283 万）。8 张 RTX 3060 固定计数加速比 7.487（通用执行配置）和 5.605（稳定配置）；弱扩展实际工作吞吐增益 6.113 和 7.295。不同任务/硬件间不构成强扩展性对比。

Conclusion: 分布式束搜索在正确性条件下可扩展到多 GPU 并保持单机等价结果，实测在大规模 B 值下实现高吞吐与近线性加速。但目标谜题（深度 9）未解，且跨任务/硬件结果不直接证明强扩展性。历史实现存在未复现的潜在缺陷，需后续版本逐一验证。

Abstract: Beam search repeatedly makes many children, removes duplicates, and keeps the best $B$. We show how many GPUs can perform these steps as one search even when the retained set does not fit on one device. States and candidates stay on the GPUs; the CPU receives only small control and ancestry records. We prove that an abstract distributed pipeline returns the monolithic reduced-key top-$B$ for the same complete candidates, integer scores, Hash128 equivalence, and a fully specified physical-layout tie order, provided that its implementation performs one global reduction per key and race-free routing. A static audit of the historical one-T4 pin leaves cross-buffer uniqueness unresolved and identifies a separate multi-rank scatter risk; neither is a reproduced failure, and other revisions inherit neither defects nor correctness without comparison. On eight H200 GPUs, a Cube4 run completed saturated depth 8 at $B_{\rm eff}=2{,}900{,}361{,}216$ in $931.266$ s: $69{,}608{,}669{,}184$ nominal parent--generator pairs, or a derived $74.746$ million pairs/s. The puzzle remained unsolved and depth 9 was stopped at user request. A separate two-T4 Megaminx run measured $30.274$ million logical children/s at $B_{\rm eff}=82{,}837{,}504$. Those tasks and hardware differ, so they do not establish strong scaling. Separately, on one eight-RTX-3060 host, fixed-count eight-GPU speedup was $7.487$ under a common execution profile and $5.605$ under selected stable profiles; weak actual-work throughput gain was $6.113$ and $7.295$. These profile-sensitive ratios use each series' own one-GPU baseline.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [PonyTail: Profiling and Improving Service Tail Latency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04615)
*Roee Wodislawski,Adam Morrison*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 本文提出了PonyTail，一种针对尾部服务时间的分析方法与工具链，用于发现导致服务时延异常的控制流和微架构模式，并通过优化尾部服务时间显著降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据中心服务中，当请求调度接近最优时，进一步降低尾部延迟的关键在于缩短服务时间。但传统热点优化难以覆盖广泛分散的服务时间，故需针对尾部服务时间的异常行为进行定位。

Method: 构建PonyTail性能分析方法和工具箱，收集并分析服务级别异常（尤其是尾部服务时间）对应的控制流路径和微架构事件，定位导致服务时间异常的根本原因，并指导针对性优化。

Result: 将PonyTail应用于五个延迟敏感服务（含内存数据库和在线搜索引擎），基于其分析进行优化后，99th percentile服务时间下降10.7%–46%，整体尾部延迟降低21%–5倍。

Conclusion: 针对尾部服务时间的离散特性进行专项分析与优化是一种有效的途径，能够在调度已近最优的情况下继续显著改善数据中心服务的尾延迟。

Abstract: Datacenter services must meet tight tail latency service-level objectives. Research on improving tail latency focuses on reducing queuing delay by approximating optimal request scheduling policies. As request scheduling nears optimality, the primary way to further improve tail latency is to reduce the service time of requests. Unfortunately, datacenter services lack obvious hotspots for general service time optimization.
  We point out a new optimization opportunity in targeting the tail service time of services with dispersive service times, which are common in datacenters. To show the feasibility and benefit of this approach, we present PonyTail, a performance analysis methodology and toolkit for analyzing service-level outlier behaviors associated with tail service time. PonyTail helps performance engineers identify control-flow and microarchitectural patterns responsible for service time outliers, as well as their root causes.
  We apply PonyTail to analyze five latency-critical services, including an in-memory database system and an online search engine. Based on PonyTail's analysis, we optimize some of the services, improving their 99th percentile service time and latency by 10.7%--46% and 21%--$5\times$, respectively.

</details>


### [110] [Energy-performance tradeoffs in server farms with batch services and setup times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04881)
*Thu Le-Anh,Tuan Phung-Duc*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 本文提出一种批处理队列模型M/M/c/SET--BATCH，通过服务器批量处理作业来最小化能耗，同时保持可接受的性能。研究分析了该模型及其变体（如允许空闲服务器延迟关闭、动态批量大小）的稳态概率和性能。在较低计算复杂度下完成分析，利用了模型的特殊结构，并通过仿真比较了不同设置时间分布下的变体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心能耗巨大，其中大量由于空闲服务器导致的能源浪费。直接关闭空闲服务器存在能省性能之间的权衡问题，亟需一种既能降低能耗又能维持系统性能的方法。

Method: 建立M/M/c/SET--BATCH多服务器批服务 queueing 模型，考虑固定批量大小和设置时间，以及两个变体：允许空闲前延迟关闭的系统和动态批量大小系统。利用模型特殊结构推导稳态概率和性能分析，降低计算复杂度。采用仿真方法比较不同设置时间分布下模型及变体的性能。

Result: 分析获得M/M/c/SET--BATCH系统及其变体的稳态概率与性能指标。仿真结果表明，当设置时间具有较大变异系数时，M/M/c/SET--BATCH模型性能更优。批量处理策略能有效提升系统性能，尤其当允许服务器在关闭前处于空闲状态时效果更明显。

Conclusion: 所提批处理策略能有效降低数据中心能耗，同时保持系统性能；在设置时间波动较大时表现尤为突出。允许空闲延迟关闭机制有助于进一步提升性能，是能源节省与性能优化的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Data centers consume a large amount of energy, much of which is wasted due to idle servers. Turning off idle servers might be an effective power-saving solution; however, there is a trade-off between energy savings and system performance. Hence, we propose a setup queueing model with a batching policy that allows servers to process a set of jobs simultaneously to minimize power consumption while maintaining acceptable performance. We consider an M/M/c/SET--BATCH queue, a multi-server batch service queue with a fixed batch size and setup times, and some variants, including systems in which idle servers delay before turning off or systems in which the batch size is dynamic. We analyze the steady-state probabilities and system performance of the M/M/c/SET--BATCH system and its variants. Our analysis of the M/M/c/SET--BATCH system with lower computational complexity is made possible by utilizing the special structure of the model. In addition, we use simulations to compare the M/M/c/SET--BATCH model with some other variants with different setup time distributions. The results suggest that the model performs better when the setup time has a larger coefficient of variation. Our results indicate that the batching policy enhances the system performance, especially when we allow servers to be idle before turning them off.

</details>


### [111] [LLBPE: Linked-List Based GPU-Parallel BPE Tokenizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04951)
*Aditya Kovilur,Varun Chandra Shekar,Ariful Azad*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了LLBPE，一种用于GPU加速的字节对编码（BPE）分词方法，通过优化数据结构和内核融合，显著提高了吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是解决现有GPU分词实现中数据移动成为瓶颈的问题，提高大语言模型推理的效率。

Method: 方法包括将标记序列表示为基于数组的链表，以实现常数时间的指针更新，并将秩查找、最小值选择和合并操作融合到一个内核中。

Result: 结果实现了比最佳GPU实现高5.2倍、比优化CPU实现高24.6倍的吞吐量，但输出存在轻微差异。

Conclusion: 结论是LLBPE在牺牲轻微准确性的代价下，大幅提升了分词吞吐量，适用于对速度要求高的场景。

Abstract: Every LLM inference begins with tokenization, which converts raw input bytes into the discrete token sequence the model consumes. For text, this step is often implemented using Byte Pair Encoding (BPE), an algorithm originally introduced for data compression. BPE has traditionally run on the CPU with extensive optimization, but recent work has moved it to the GPU for higher throughput. We show that these GPU implementations are bottlenecked not by computation but by data movement. We develop LLBPE that represents the token sequence as an array-based linked list so that each merge reduces to a constant- time pointer update. Furthermore, LLBPE fuses rank lookup, minimum selection, and merging into a single kernel to eliminate redundant hash map queries. LLBPE achieves up to 5.2x higher throughput than the best existing GPU implementation and 24.6x over optimized CPU implementations, at the cost of minor discrepancies in tokenized output.

</details>
